For fundamentally different output spaces, the paper recommends moving to full-network or internal-layer comparisons instead of head error geometry.41:T4d8,Instead of relying on binary "behavior present / behavior absent" contrastive pairs, the method uses an iterative generation process to produce a sequence of samples with graded behavioral intensity: starting from a neutral seed response, the model is iteratively steered to produce responses that move incrementally towards and away from the target behavior, generating a continuous spectrum of samples from fully neutral to extreme behavioral expression. SAE features are extracted across this sample sequence, and only features that show monotonic linear activation scaling with behavioral intensity are retained as cascading linear features. This monotonicity criterion filters out spurious, incidentally correlated features and retains features with causal relevance to the behavior, a logic loosely aligned with slow feature analysis which prioritizes smoothly varying, stable signals over transient noise. Behavioral intensity is quantified as a linear sum of retained feature activations, producing a continuous, deterministic metric. Steering is performed by adjusting only these targeted feature activations, rather than manipulating the full dense residual stream, substantially reducing entanglement with unrelated capabilities.42:T45f,The diagnostic separates two decoding modes. A CoT action is produced after the model generates a reasoning trace. A prompt action is produced by prefilling an <action> tag, forcing the model to skip the reasoning trace and predict the action directly from the prompt. If training mainly improves reasoning-based revision, the gap between CoT actions and prompt actions should grow over training. If training also improves the direct prompt-to-action pathway, prompt actions should improve in parallel and the gap may remain stable. The paper then uses conflicting-trace tests, where the provided reasoning trace conflicts with the prompt-implied action, to measure whether the final action follows the prompt or the trace. Attention and gradient analyses are used to explain the behavior: the prompt is usually much longer than the generated CoT and carries task instructions, history, and observations, so action-token supervision can assign more signal to prompt-conditioned representations. This motivates a reduced action-supervision intervention that weakens final-action supervision on part of the training data.43:T44d,The experiments cover ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and BFCL, spanning embodied household tasks, interactive science tasks, and function-calling or tool-use evaluation. Both supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning are considered. The main empirical pattern is consistent across settings: prompt-action quality rises substantially during training, while the relative advantage of CoT actions over prompt actions does not widen much. CoT still helps, but the training gain is not concentrated only in reasoning-mediated action changes. In conflicting-trace tests, later checkpoints are harder to overturn with traces that disagree with the prompt, indicating stronger anchoring to prompt-implied actions. Attention and gradient measurements support the same explanation: action-time signal is dominated by the long prompt more than by the generated trace. Reducing action-token supervision on a subset of examples improves out-of-distribution generalization. The visible text does not provide the full numeric tables, so the exact gains should be checked in the main experiment section and appendix.44:T8b4,The method has three layers.

First, the paper performs a mechanistic comparison between Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Foundation-Sec-8B-Instruct. The key claim is that Foundation-Sec inherits an existing mid-layer classification route, highlighted around Layer 12, rather than learning a wholly new detector. The evidence comes from causal interventions and representation analysis: the authors examine how output confidence changes when internal activations are ablated or substituted, instead of relying only on descriptive attention patterns.

Second, they introduce the indicator-token sign test. For a malicious script family, they identify canonical indicator tokens such as Invoke-WebRequest. They then ablate those tokens from canonical malicious scripts and compare the change in malicious-class confidence between the base model and the fine-tuned model. If removing the token decreases malicious confidence in the base model, the token acts as a driver there. If removing the same token increases malicious confidence in the fine-tuned model, the token has become a suppressor. This sign divergence is interpreted as a warning signal: the family is now classified more through surrounding payload context than through the explicit command token, making it vulnerable to behavior-preserving rewrites.

Third, they use this signal for family-level prioritization. A linear probe near the classification boundary is used to detect representation drift and rank command families for targeted red-teaming. The benchmark is then built in three tiers: direct rewrites, command reconstruction, and case mutation, with matched seed/variant pairs and syntax or manual validation to preserve behavior. This is an important design choice. The benchmark is not just an obfuscation leaderboard. It isolates transformation classes so failures can be attributed to specific semantic rewrites.

Compared with standard security evaluation, the novelty is not a larger attack set. It is a pre-deployment monitoring method grounded in internal representation changes. Compared with generic OOD detection, it relies on causal role changes of indicator tokens in a base/fine-tuned model pair, not just confidence shifts or embedding distance.45:T749,The experiments use a matched cohort of 293 PowerShell script pairs and a three-tier evasion benchmark, comparing Foundation-Sec-8B-Instruct against its lineage-matched base model, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. Three results matter most.

First, the indicator-token sign test predicts the risky family before any evasion variants are tried. Invoke-WebRequest shows the clearest sign divergence, and the benchmark later confirms consistent misses on the iwr alias substitution. That makes the diagnostic predictive rather than merely post hoc.

Second, the failure is transformation-sensitive rather than a general loss of classification ability. Foundation-Sec improves canonical classification behavior, and the paper cites prior reporting of roughly a +16% precision gain over baseline in a threat-hunting workflow. Yet it misses malicious scripts under several behavior-preserving rewrites, including Invoke-Expression format-string reconstruction, alternating-case Invoke-Expression, and alternating-case IEX alias variants. Llama records zero misses on the same evaluated variant sets. This contrast is the core empirical point: fine-tuning improved the nominal task while removing robustness that the base model still had.

Third, prompt-level remediation is unstable. Fixing one family through prompting can open or amplify misses in another family. That suggests the issue is not a shallow prompt artifact but a deeper reorganization of internal decision semantics. The excerpt does not expose every table value, but the paper clearly combines at least three forms of evidence: family-level sign-test ranking, tiered evasion evaluation, and causal intervention on internal routes. When reading the full paper, the most important quantitative artifacts are the Layer-12 route analysis, the linear-probe family ranking, and the miss breakdown by transformation type.46:T736,Read it in four passes: problem framing, mechanism, diagnostic, and benchmark validation.

Start with the introduction and lock onto the main claim. The paper is not saying all security fine-tuning reduces robustness. It is saying standard held-out evaluation misses a fine-tuning-induced class of transformation-sensitive evasion. The driver-versus-suppressor distinction is the conceptual anchor for the rest of the paper.

Then move to the mechanistic analysis. Focus on how the authors support the claim that Foundation-Sec inherits Llama’s existing classification route rather than learning a new detector. Any activation patching, causal intervention, or layer-wise effect plots deserve close attention, especially the evidence around Layer 12. If that section only showed correlation, the paper would be much weaker; the value comes from causal evidence.

Next, read the definition of the sign test and the linear-probe ranking carefully. Two questions matter: how indicator tokens are selected, and why sign reversal is a reliable family-level risk signal rather than a token artifact. If the paper reports ranking quality or precision-style metrics for family prioritization, those are worth checking closely because they determine whether the method is usable in practice.

Finally, study the benchmark construction and the case studies. The most informative families are Invoke-WebRequest/iwr, Invoke-Expression format-string reconstruction, and case mutation. They represent direct aliasing, structural rewriting, and surface-form perturbation. If time is limited, reading the introduction, the sign-test method, and the family-level case studies should take about 25 to 35 minutes. For a deeper understanding of how fine-tuning reallocates internal semantics, read the mechanistic section and appendix intervention details as well.3:["$","main",null,{"className":"flex-1 overflow-y-auto","children":["$","div",null,{"className":"mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 xl:max-w-6xl","children":["$","$L9",null,{"data":{"date":"2026-06-29","generated_at":"2026-06-29T01:45:50.010450+00:00","stats":{"total":119,"starred":4,"must_read":7,"skim":58,"glance":54,"by_category":{"pretraining_data":2,"industry":3,"llm_pretrain":2,"scaling":2,"architecture":6,"efficient":17,"eval":15,"multimodal":9,"multimodal_gen":10,"post_train":16,"mechanism":21,"coding_agent":1,"agent":15},"trending_repos":5,"trending_hf":10,"fetch_status":{}},"categories":[{"id":"pretraining_data","emoji":"📚","name":"预训练数据","description":"以预训练数据为核心贡献的工作：数据采集/清洗/去重、数据配比(data mixture)、数据筛选/质量评估、语料构建、数据课程(data curriculum)、数据重复(repetition)、合成数据用于预训练、Tokenizer/词表设计、成员推断/数据检测。判据是『论文核心是数据本身』，而非训练 recipe/优化器(→ llm_pretrain)或评测资产(→ eval)","papers":[{"id":"2606.27019","title":"MinGram: A Minimalist Unigram Tokenizer with High Compression and Competitive Morphological Alignment","abstract":"The Unigram tokenizer uses an elegant representation which makes it straightforward to edit vocabularies, but its training is comparatively heavy and complex. It tries to keep the editable token-list representation of Unigram while making training closer to BPE-level simplicity and preserving strong compression.","importance":"The main relevance is tokenizer training for pretraining pipelines. MinGram is not a new LM architecture; it is a simpler way to obtain a Unigram-style vocabulary that can be edited, pruned, merged, or repaired more easily than a BPE merge list. This matters because tokenizer changes are expensive once pretraining starts, and recent work has shown several BPE failure modes around undertrained tokens, morphology, and vocabulary inefficiency. MinGram offers a practical point in the trade-off: prioritize fewer tokens per text, but retain a lightweight probabilistic score to avoid the morphology degradation seen in pure least-token segmentation.","technical_approach":"MinGram replaces three heavy components of standard Unigram training. It uses an oversized BPE vocabulary as the seed instead of suffix-array substring mining. It replaces marginal EM over the segmentation lattice with Hard EM over a single minimum-token path. For a sequence x, it chooses a segmentation minimizing |s| - δ·sum log p(s_j), so token count is the primary objective and the Unigram score acts as a tie-break. It then counts tokens on the selected paths and re-estimates probabilities. Finally, it uses one flat score-based pruning step instead of iterative loss-estimate pruning. Compared with PathPiece or pure least-token inference, the method keeps a probability signal. Compared with standard SentencePiece Unigram, it gives up some probabilistic completeness for a much simpler implementation.","experiments":"The paper reports comparisons across six languages, focusing on compression, morphological alignment, and downstream quality. Dataset Usage Inference (DUI) aims to answer this by estimating what fraction of a dataset contributed to a model's training. These conditions make curre","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-24T18:02:42Z","category_id":"pretraining_data","cover_url":"/covers/2606_26257.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"如何在不训练昂贵的影子模型（shadow models）且没有可信的、未参与训练的保留数据集（held-out data）的情况下，推断某个数据集有多大比例被用于训练目标生成模型（Dataset Usage Inference, DUI）？","importance":"为大模型时代的数据版权和合规审计提供了一种切实可行的方案。传统的 DUI 方法（如 DUCI）依赖影子模型和保留数据，这在动辄数十亿参数的现代生成模型面前计算成本极高且不切实际。本文提出的 NU-DUI 框架将计算成本降低了三个数量级，使得对大型自回归或扩散模型的训练数据审计成为可能。","technical_approach":"将 DUI 转化为混合比例估计（Mixture Proportion Estimation, MPE）问题。核心创新在于构建合成的非成员参考集：1) 使用与目标模型不同架构的生成器对可疑数据集进行图像到图像（img2img）的重写，生成合成的非成员样本；2) 使用重写过程中相同的自编码器对可疑数据集进行自编码，以消除生成器伪影带来的分布偏移，使得成员和非成员之间的差异主要由是否参与训练决定；3) 提取针对目标模型家族定制的成员推断攻击（MIA）特征；4) 应用 MPE 估计成员比例。","experiments":"在五个大型图像生成模型（包括自回归和扩散模型，如 RAR-XXL）上进行了验证。在 RAR-XXL 上，对于 1000 张图像的可疑集，NU-DUI 仅需 42.5 分钟（A100），而传统方法需要 1500 多小时，加速比超过 2000 倍。同时，估计误差（MAE）保持在 0.1 以下（例如 0.058），接近需要可信保留数据的理想情况。","reading_guide":"建议重点阅读 Method 部分，特别是如何通过 img2img 重写和自编码对齐来构建合成非成员参考集的设计，这是本文最巧妙的地方。实验部分可以关注其在不同模型架构（自回归 vs 扩散）上的鲁棒性表现。预计阅读时间 15 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"How to infer the fraction of a dataset used to train a target generative model (Dataset Usage Inference, DUI) without training expensive shadow models or requiring a trusted held-out dataset?","importance":"Provides a practical solution for data copyright and compliance auditing in the era of large models. su","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-25T15:23:03Z","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2606_27153.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.DC","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"矩阵正交化优化器 Muon 在收敛效率上相比 AdamW 有显著优势，但其 Newton-Schulz 迭代作用于完整权重矩阵，与当前基于逐元素优化假设构建的分布式训练栈（参数按 shard 分置于各 rank）存在结构性失配。每一步需要重建完整矩阵带来的额外通信与计算开销，使原生 Muon 实现的 optimizer step 耗时超过前向+反向的总和，抵消了其 FLOP 级收敛优势，阻碍了大规模训练中的实际部署。","importance":"首次系统解决分布式场景下矩阵感知优化器的系统开销问题，将 Muon 的实际 step 开销压缩到接近 AdamW 的水平，使 Muon 的收敛收益能真正转化为 wall-clock 端到端训练加速。方案已在 WALL-OSS-0.5、WALL-WM 等生产级任务中验证，对预训练优化器栈的下一代演进（从逐元素到矩阵感知）有直接的工程落地价值，也为 FSDP/ZeRO 与非逐元素优化器的兼容设计提供了明确方向。","technical_approach":"DMuon 是一个可即插即用的分布式 Muon 模块，与宿主训练框架、并行策略解耦，核心设计分四层：1) owner-centric 通信优化：为每个完整矩阵选定一个 owner rank，非 owner 把各自 shard 通过一次 all-gather 聚合到 owner，在 owner 上执行完整 Newton-Schulz 迭代后再把更新后的 shard 分发回去，通信量最小且保留精确优化器语义；避免了逐 shard 计算无法正交化的根本问题。2) 形状自适应执行栈：采用批量 Gram Newton-Schulz 执行（先算 M M^T / M^T M 再迭代，减少运算量）、对称感知专用 kernel（利用 Gram 矩阵对称减半计算）、DSL 驱动的自动调优，在异构形状的大量矩阵上都能达到高算力利用率。3) 计算感知负载均衡：将 owner 分配建模为基于实测耗时的优化问题，结合 batching、kernel 选择、自动调优带来的非线性开销，在运行时 profile 后做全局分配，避免 owner 之间负载不均。4) 即插即用接口：对现有训练管线只暴露标准优化器调用，底层兼容 ZeRO、FSDP2、张量并行等多种切分方式，不改训练主循环。与原生 Muon 相比，DMuon 不做任何算法近似，保持完全相同的更新规则与收敛行为。","experiments":"在三类任务上验证：1) 机器人 VLA 训练（WALL-OSS-0.5、WALL-WM）：VLA 任务前向反向计算占比低，optimizer 开销问题最突出。DMuon 实现了接近 AdamW 的 optimizer step 开销（差距通常在 10%–20% 以内），而原生 Muon 是 AdamW 的 2 倍以上；端到端 step 时间相比原生 Muon 显著降低，同时完整保留 Muon 的收敛曲线与最终性能。2) 语言模型预训练：不同参数规模、张量并行宽度下，DMuon 的额外开销相比 AdamW 控制在小范围，在大模型宽并行设置下通信优化的收益更明显。3) 消融实验逐项验证 owner 选择策略、批量 Gram 执行、对称 kernel、自动调优各自的贡献，其中 owner-centric 通信与批量 Gram Newton-Schulz 是两项最大的单点收益来源。论文给出的核心结论是，Muon 在 FLOP 层面的收敛优势可以通过 DMuon 无损耗地转化为 wall-clock 层面的实际训练加速。","reading_guide":"精读。先看 §2 背景部分，彻底理清 Muon 的 Newton-Schulz 迭代输入是完整矩阵这一特性，以及它与 ZeRO / FSDP / TP 三种切分方式分别冲突在哪里——这是整篇论文设计的根因。再读 §3 四层技术方案：重点读图 2 的 owner-centric 数据流，理解为什么选 owner + all-gather 比跨 rank 协同做 Newton-Schulz 更简洁高效；§3.2 的 Gram Newton-Schulz 推导与对称 kernel 是性能翻倍的关键；§3.3 的负载均衡建模思路有通用参考价值。§4 实验部分重点看不同任务下 optimizer step 时间、端到端 step 时间相对于 AdamW 和原生 Muon 的柱状对比，以及参数规模/并行宽度对开销的影响曲线。附录里的 kernel 细节与自动调优 DSL 对工程实现有直接价值。值得深挖的点：这套框架对其他矩阵感知优化器（如 Shampoo、SOAP 变体）的可迁移性，以及 MoE 场景下大量专家矩阵的 owner 分配策略。","authors_note":"WALL 团队与伯克利相关作者（含 Moonlight、WALL-OSS 系列工作的主要作者群），涉及机器人基础模型与大规模训练系统方向。"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Matrix-orthogonalizing optimizers like Muon provide clear convergence advantages over AdamW, but their Newton-Schulz iteration requires operating on full weight matrices. This is fundamentally mismatched with modern distributed training stacks built around the element-wise optimizer assumption, where parameters are split into shards across ranks. Reconstructing full matrices at every optimizer step introduces heavy communication and computation overhead: naive Muon implementations often spend more time in the optimizer step than in the combined forward and backward passes, erasing Muon's FLOP-level convergence advantage and blocking its large-scale deployment.","importance":"Presents the first systematic solution to the systems overhead of matrix-aware optimizers in distributed settings, compressing Muon's real step overhead to a level close to AdamW. As a result, Muon's convergence gains translate directly into wall-clock end-to-end training speedups. Validated in production-grade workloads including WALL-OSS-0.5 and WALL-WM, the work has immediate engineering value for the next-generation optimizer stack (element-wise to matrix-aware) and provides a clear template for making FSDP / ZeRO compatible with non-element-wise update rules.","technical_approach":"$c","experiments":"Validated across three classes of workloads. (1) Robotics VLA training (WALL-OSS-0.5, WALL-WM): VLA tasks have low forward/backward compute density, so optimizer overhead is most severe. DMuon reaches near-AdamW optimizer step cost (typically within 10%–20%), while naive Muon exceeds 2x AdamW cost. End-to-end step time is significantly reduced against naive Muon, with Muon's convergence curve and final quality fully preserved. (2) Language model pretraining: across parameter scales and tensor-parallel widths, DMuon's additional overhead over AdamW stays in a small range, with larger gains in large-model wide-parallel configurations where communication optimization matters most. (3) Ablations quantify the contribution of each component: owner-centric communication and batched Gram Newton-Schulz are the two largest single-point gains. The core result is that Muon's FLOP-level convergence advantage is converted, without loss, into actual wall-clock training speedup via DMuon.","reading_guide":"$d","authors_note":"WALL team together with Berkeley-affiliated authors, including the main contributors to the Moonlight and WALL-OSS series, working on robotics foundation models and large-scale training systems."},"recommendation":{"zh":"应立刻看实现细节并复现实验；Muon 的瓶颈若真被系统化解，优化器 ablation 的成本结构会变。","en":"Read the implementation details and reproduce it; if Muon’s overhead is really systemically removed, optimizer ablation economics change."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Vincent Chen","Starrick Liu","Regis Cheng","Dance Yang","Shalfun Li","Ryan Yu","Lucy Liang","Hang Su","Roy Gan","Hao Wang","Qian Wang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["Muon","optimizer","distributed-training","Newton-Schulz","LLM-pretraining"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27153","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.27153","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.27216","title":"Hierarchical Muon: Tiled Newton-Schulz Updates for Efficient Muon Optimization","abstract":"Muon-type optimizers construct update directions for dense neural-network weights by applying a finite Newton-Schulz map to momentum-gradient matrices. For an $H \\times W$ matrix, with $r=\\min\\{H,W\\}$ and $s=\\max\\{H,W\\}$, $K$ steps of the full-matrix Newton-Schulz update require $O(r^2 s K)$ work and couple all rows and columns through repeated Gram matrix products. We introduce Hierarchical Muon (HiMuon), a tiled Newton-Schulz scheme for Muon-type optimization. HiMuon partitions each momentum-g","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-25T16:05:55Z","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2606_27216.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["math.NA","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"标准Muon优化器的全矩阵Newton-Schulz更新计算复杂度高（O(r²sK)），存在全局谱耦合，分布式参数分片训练下还需要额外集合通信重构完整矩阵，制约了大模型预训练阶段的优化器执行效率。","importance":"为预训练优化器设计提供了谱耦合范围与计算开销可显式权衡的新思路，HiMuon的分块实现可直接降低Transformer预训练的优化器时延，且原生适配分布式参数分片布局，对大模型训练系统优化有实际工程参考价值。","technical_approach":"提出分层Muon（HiMuon），将每个动量-梯度矩阵划分为T×T的分块网格，对每个分块独立执行K步Newton-Schulz迭代后重组输出。分块大小T同时作为数值参数（控制保留的谱耦合范围）和计算参数（控制Newton-Schulz迭代开销）。实现层面针对分块后的批量小矩阵运算做了定制化kernel、跨层批处理、内存受限分块、运行时分块大小调度，且支持分布式场景下各rank本地完成分块计算，无需额外跨分片通信。","experiments":"在矩阵函数层、单层级别、优化器步级别、端到端训练四个层级完成评估，覆盖Transformer真实权重形状、kernel与分块形状选型、跨层批处理收益、有限精度下Newton-Schulz计算与训练质量的权衡，还引入关联记忆基准作为分块大小选择的结构诊断工具。核心结论是当T远小于矩阵最小维度时，Newton-Schulz计算的主导复杂度大幅下降，且可在训练质量与速度间取得平滑的可调权衡。","reading_guide":"重点关注第4章HiMuon算法与GPU实现细节、第6章的效率与训练质量消融实验。预计阅读时间15-20分钟，若已熟悉Muon优化器基础可跳过第2章预备知识部分。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The full-matrix Newton-Schulz (NS) update in standard Muon optimizers has high computational complexity (O(r²sK)), introduces global spectral coupling across all matrix entries, and requires extra collective communication to reconstruct full matrices in distributed parameter-sharded training, limiting optimizer throughput during large-scale LLM pretraining.","importance":"Introduces an explicit, tunable tradeoff between spectral coupling scope and computational cost for pretraining optimizers. HiMuon's tiled implementation directly reduces optimizer latency in Transformer pretraining and natively supports distributed parameter-sharded layouts, delivering practical engineering insights for large-scale training system optimization.","technical_approach":"Proposes Hierarchical Muon (HiMuon), which partitions each momentum-gradient matrix into a T×T tile grid, applies K-step NS iterations independently to each tile, then reassembles the outputs. Tile size T acts as both a numerical hyperparameter (controlling the retained spectral coupling range) and a computational parameter (controlling NS iteration cost). The implementation includes tile-size-specific kernels, cross-layer batching, memory-bounded chunking, runtime tile-size scheduling, and support for local per-rank tiled computation in distributed settings without cross-shard communication overhead.","experiments":"Evaluations are conducted at four levels: matrix-function, per-layer, optimizer-step, and end-to-end training. Experiments cover real Transformer weight shapes, kernel/tile shape selection, cross-layer batching gains, finite-precision NS computation tradeoffs with training quality, plus an associative-memory benchmark as a structural diagnostic for tile size selection. The key finding is that when T is far smaller than the minimum matrix dimension, the leading-order NS computation cost drops significantly, with a smooth, tunable tradeoff between training quality and throughput.","reading_guide":"Focus on Section 4 (HiMuon algorithm and GPU implementation details) and Section 6 (ablation studies on efficiency and training quality). 模型上。作者报告的总体结果是，平均成功率最高提升 33.14%，平均提升 13.52%。从摘要和正文片段看，这个提升不是单一环境偶然出现，而是在多环境、多 embodiment 下都能复现。\n\n更有信息量的是消融方向。作者专门分析了 reasoning scaling 和 action scaling feedback 机制则提升了采样效率，避免在低质量候选上浪费过多预算。\n\n不过从基座研究角度，也要看到实验边界：这里的收益主要是 inference-time orchestration 带来的，不是模型参数或预训练数据层面的改进。若精读全文，建议重点找四类数字：不同 VLA backbone 上的增益是否稳定；不同环境中增益是否集中在长程任务；reasoning-only、action-only、joint 三种 scaling 的对比；以及迭代轮数/候选数增加后的收益曲线，看看 compute-performance trade-off 是否平滑。","reading_guide":"如果只是判断是否值得跟进，先看 Introduction 和方法总览图，确认它解决的是 paper addresses two missing pieces in embodied test-time scaling. Existing methods mostly scale action selection only, without scaling high-level reasoning, and they often evaluate candidates from the current observation alone, ignoring trajectory history. That is a poor fit for robotic manipulation, where tasks are sequential, long-horizon, and often require iterative correction. The question the paper asks is simple: without retraining the policy or collecting more expert data, can extra inference-time compute be converted into higher manipulation success by jointly scaling reasoning and action, while making verification history-aware and refinement iterative?","importance":"For core LLM pretraining research, this is not central. It is primarily an embodied/VLA inference-time framework rather than a pretraining contribution. Still, it offers two useful ideas. First, it treats reasoning itself as a target of test-time scaling instead of only reranking final actions. That parallels the shift in language models from scaling answer sampling to scaling intermediate reasoning trajectories. Second, it argues that the verifier must consume history, not just the current state. In sequential decision problems, the bottleneck is not only how many candidates are sampled, but whether the evaluator has enough temporal context to judge them. That lesson is relevant to agents, long-horizon planning, and tool-using LM systems. We propose TwoTower, a block-wise autoregressive diffusion model that decouples these roles into two towers: a frozen AR context tower that causally processes clean toke","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-25T00:52:44Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2606_26493.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是离散 diffusion LM 在大模型尺度上的一个结构性矛盾：同一套参数既要承担“干净上下文的表示器”，又要承担“带噪 token 的迭代去噪器”。这两个角色的注意力结构、信息流方向、训练信号都不同。前者更像标准 AR LM，需要稳定地压缩和传递历史语义；后者则要在每个 diffusion step 上对局部噪声块做条件重建，还希望块内能双向看见彼此。把两者绑在一个 decoder 里，会让模型在表示 clean context 和 denoise noisy block 之间相互牵制。论文的核心问题不是再做一个小型 diffusion decoder，而是验证：在 30B 级、混合 Mamba/Attention/MoE 骨干上，能否把这两个角色彻底拆开，并借助一个已经很强的预训练 AR 模型作为 frozen context tower，让 diffusion tower 只学去噪，从而在质量接近 AR 基线的前提下换取更高吞吐。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义有两层。第一，它给 diffusion LM 提供了一条比“从头训练单塔去噪模型”更现实的路线：直接复用成熟 AR 预训练骨干，把 AR 预训练能力当作条件表示器，再追加一个专门的 denoiser。这样做把 AR 预训练资产转成了 diffusion 生成资产，降低了新范式落地门槛。第二，它说明在大规模混合架构上，context modeling 与 denoising 的解耦不是只在 1B 级别成立，而是可以扩展到 30B MoE，并且只用约 2.1T token 的额外训练，就能保住大部分 AR 质量。这对“AR 是否会被并行生成范式部分替代”给出了更工程化的答案：短期内更可行的路径不是完全抛弃 AR，而是把 AR 变成条件塔、把 diffusion 变成生成塔。贡献类型偏架构与训练范式，量级属于值得精读的范式性工程工作。","technical_approach":"$12","experiments":"论文最重要的实验结论有三个。第一，规模与训练预算上，这不是小模型验证。底座是 Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B，52 层，由 23 个 Mamba-2 层、6 个 self-attention 层和 23 个 MoE 层组成。TwoTower 在这个 30B 混合 MoE 骨干上训练，额外训练 token 约 2.1T，而原始骨干预训练总量约 25T。也就是说，作者不是从头重训一个 diffusion LM，而是在强 AR 基座上做相对节制的追加训练。\n\n第二，质量-速度权衡是这篇工作的主结果。作者报告 TwoTower 在保留大部分 AR 基线质量的同时，获得了明显更高的解码吞吐。机制上，默认采样点会在早期 refinement step 一次性提交多个 token，而不是像 AR 那样每步只前进 1 个 token，这解释了 wall-clock speedup 的来源。论文摘要截断处已明确写到“preserves most of the autoregressive baseline’s quality while delivering substantially higher throughput”；正文也强调速度收益来自 block-wise parallel commit，而不是单步 FLOPs 更低。\n\n第三，消融实验支持其关键设计。作者在 3.2 节对若干改动做了对比：time conditioning 明显提升采样质量；layer-wise 对齐访问 context 表示优于只用末层条件；bidirectional Mamba 只带来边际收益，不值得额外 FLOPs；因此最终版本只保留 bidirectional attention。还有一个重要工程观察是，context tower 冻结后仍能提供足够强的条件表示，说明额外训练主要集中在“如何利用已有 AR 表示做去噪”，而不是重新学习语言知识。\n\n受限于给出的全文片段，具体 benchmark 数字、吞吐倍数、不同 block size / denoising step 的最优点没有完整展开；但从方法描述和实验总结可以确定，这篇工作的说服力主要来自 30B 级真实训练、2.1T token 追加预算、以及质量接近 AR 基线同时换来更高吞吐这组三元结果。","reading_guide":"建议先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1，把问题设定吃透：作者不是在做“更强的 masked LM”，而是在做“AR context + diffusion denoiser”的职责拆分。随后重点看 Method 2.1 和算法描述，理解 block-wise autoregressive generation 的状态流：哪些状态由 context tower 持久化，哪些状态只在当前 block 内存在。\n\n精读时最值得盯的点有四个。第一，layer-aligned cross-attention 的实现细节和动机。这是整篇最有信息量的设计，因为它把预训练 AR 表示当成多尺度条件，而不是单一语义摘要。第二，Mamba state 如何从 context tower 注入 denoiser。对混合架构研究者，这部分比普通 Transformer 版 diffusion 更有参考价值。第三，3.2 节消融。重点看 time conditioning、bidirectional attention、bidirectional Mamba、末层条件 vs 逐层条件的对比，这些实验决定了哪些设计是真有效，哪些只是看起来合理。第四，推理部分关于 throughput、block size、denoising steps、提交策略的分析。真正决定这类模型是否有部署价值的，不是 perplexity，而是 wall-clock 与质量曲线。\n\n如果时间有限，优先顺序可以是：Figure 1 + Algorithm 1，Method 2.1/2.2，Ablation 3.2，主结果表和速度图。预计 45-60 分钟能抓住主干；若要深挖，建议进一步思考两个问题：为什么冻结 context tower 仍然够用；以及这种 two-tower 设计能否迁移到更纯粹的 any-order LM 或多模态 token 生成。","authors_note":"NVIDIA 工作。文中明确基于 Nemotron 系列开放权重模型展开，属于 NVIDIA 在 Nemotron 预训练体系上的延伸。给出的片段未完整列出作者名单。"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a structural limitation in discrete diffusion language modeling at scale: a single network is usually asked to do two different jobs at once. It must represent clean causal context, and it must iteratively denoise corrupted tokens. Those roles prefer different information flow patterns and different inductive biases. Clean-context modeling looks like standard AR language modeling; denoising benefits from bidirectional access within the noisy region and from timestep-conditioned reconstruction. The paper asks whether these roles can be fully separated on top of a large modern backbone, using a frozen pretrained AR model as the context encoder and a trainable diffusion tower as the denoiser, while keeping most of the AR model’s quality and gaining decoding throughput.","importance":"This matters for pretraining research because it offers a practical migration path from AR pretraining to diffusion-style generation. Instead of training a large diffusion LM from scratch, the work reuses a strong pretrained AR backbone as a conditional representation engine and only trains the denoising side for the new objective. That lowers the adoption barrier for non-AR generation paradigms. It also shows that the context/denoiser split is not just a small-scale architectural curiosity: it can be instantiated on a 30B-class hybrid Mamba-Transformer-MoE model and trained with about 2.1T additional tokens, far less than the 25T-token budget used for the original backbone. The contribution is mainly architectural and recipe-level, but the scale makes it relevant to anyone thinking about post-AR pretraining paradigms.","technical_approach":"$13","experiments":"$14","reading_guide":"$15","authors_note":"NVIDIA work built on the Nemotron model family. We present two efficiency methods that exploit this asymmetry. CascadeFormer tapers width with depth to match the uneven information flow across layers, achieving comparable perplexity to a uniform baseline at the same training budget while reducing latency by 8.6% and increasing throughput by 9.4%. CascadeFlow Pruning removes layers using accumulated training gradients, with no ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-25T02:25:00Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2606_26538.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解释并利用一个在深层 Transformer 里反复出现、但常被粗略归因为“深层梯度变小”的现象：越靠后的层，功能贡献往往越弱，表示也更冗余。作者给出的更精确问题定义是，问题不只是梯度幅值衰减，而是残差结构导致的梯度组合来源不对称。浅层能汇聚更多下游路径传回来的梯度信号，深层只能接收更少、更简单的组合信号。作者把这种结构性差异称为 Gradient Fan-in Asymmetry，简称 GFA。论文的核心任务有两部分：一是证明这种“梯度扇入不对称”比单纯的梯度大小更能解释深层层冗余；二是把这个解释落到两个可操作方向上，一个是训练时就内生化这种不对称的深度渐缩架构 CascadeFormer，另一个是仅依赖训练过程中累计梯度来做层裁剪的 CascadeFlow Pruning。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要有三层。第一，它试图把“深层层没那么有用”从经验观察推进到结构解释。过去常见说法是深层梯度弱、训练不到位，但这篇文章强调，优化器即便把梯度放大，也补不回缺失的组合多样性，这个判断会直接影响我们如何理解深层冗余、LayerSkip 一类深监督设计、以及深度扩展的收益边界。第二，它给出了一种和 scaling/架构设计直接相关的思路：如果不同深度天然接收到的信息密度不同，那么统一宽度堆叠未必是最合适的参数分配方式，深度越深越窄反而可能更贴近训练信号分布。第三，它提出的 pruning 信号来自训练期累计梯度，而不是训练后再做昂贵分析，这对大模型训练中的在线结构决策、mid-train 裁剪、甚至动态深度设计都有启发。贡献量级上，这不是改写主流 recipe 的工作，但它提供了一个值得认真对待的结构性解释框架，并给出两个能落地的原型方法。","technical_approach":"技术主线分三步。第一步是提出 GFA 的结构定义。对 Pre-LN 残差堆叠，作者把每层输入梯度展开为来自 identity path 和所有后续 functional path 的累积和。关键不在于完整组合路径数 2^(N-l)，而在于一个更可操作的 proxy：某层能聚合多少个不同下游信号通道，也就是 fan-in。这个 fan-in 随深度线性下降；如果再加中间层辅助监督，浅层会收到更多层级的反传信号，差异会进一步放大到近似二次级别。第二步是做相关性和干预验证。作者先测每层平均梯度范数与最终功能重要性 ΔMi 的正相关，再做两个更关键的 intervention。一个是“幅值补偿”实验：人为放大后层梯度，使其梯度大小更接近前层，但并不能恢复这些层的功能重要性。另一个是“结构补偿”实验：通过 layer repetition 增加后层可接收的路径数，结果能明显恢复其重要性。这个对照是全文最关键的论据，因为它把“梯度大小只是症状”这个说法变成了可检验命题。第三步是方法落地。CascadeFormer 不是简单减层，而是在固定训练 FLOPs 约束下做 depth-tapered width schedule：前层更宽，后层逐步变窄，让容量分配与梯度组合多样性更匹配。它保留标准 Transformer block 形式，只改每层宽度配置，因此兼容现有训练流程。CascadeFlow Pruning 则在训练过程中累计每层梯度统计，把它作为结构重要性的 proxy，直接决定裁掉哪些层。它强调不需要训练后再跑额外敏感性分析，也不依赖激活重放或复杂搜索。和常见按层位置、按权重范数、按单次梯度或按表示相似度裁剪相比，它利用的是训练全过程积累下来的“被多少有用信号持续驱动过”的信息。","experiments":"实验覆盖从头训练、最大到 1.2B 参数规模的模型。论文先给出现象层证据：在预训练后的 LLaMA 上，后层表示相似度更高；在 LayerSkip 这类可早退架构上，越后层的边际功能贡献下降更明显。真正的方法实验主要在自训模型上完成。对 CascadeFormer，作者在与 uniform baseline 保持相同训练预算的条件下比较困惑度和推理效率，结论是困惑度基本可比，同时延迟下降 8.6%，吞吐提升 9.4%。这说明 tapering 不是单纯拿质量换速度，而是在相同 train FLOPs 下改善了部署侧形状。对 CascadeFlow Pruning，作者用累计训练梯度做层重要性排序，和标准启发式相比，在 perplexity 与 rank-stability 上更好，下游精度也保持竞争力。摘要没有给出全部具体数字，但从正文叙述看，作者特别强调两点：一是 pruning 排名在不同 checkpoint 或不同评估切片上更稳定，说明信号不是偶然噪声；二是单纯看深度位置或后验表示冗余并不如训练期累计梯度可靠。消融层面，最重要的是两类干预：放大后层梯度幅值无效，而增加后层路径数有效；这组结果支撑了“结构瓶颈而非幅值瓶颈”的中心论点。另一个值得注意的观察是，深监督并没有缓解不对称，反而会把浅层的 fan-in 优势进一步放大，这对 LayerSkip 一类设计是个不太直观但很重要的提醒。","reading_guide":"建议按“假说—干预—方法”顺序读。先看 Introduction 和 GFA 定义部分，重点抓住作者到底在数什么：不是所有组合路径，而是梯度聚合的有效信号通道数。这里如果没读清，后面很容易把文章误解成普通的梯度衰减讨论。然后精读两组 intervention 实验，这是全文最有信息量的部分。要重点看：作者如何人为 equalize late-layer gradient norms；为什么这种做法没有恢复层重要性；layer repetition 又是怎样增加结构 fan-in 的。接着看 CascadeFormer 的宽度调度设计，关注它如何在固定训练 FLOPs 下做参数重分配，以及这种 schedule 是否只是手工启发式，还是由 GFA 统计直接驱动。再看 CFP 的实现细节，尤其是累计梯度的定义窗口、归一化方式、排序稳定性，以及与哪些 pruning baseline 对比。图表上，表示相似度随深度变化、LayerSkip 的层贡献曲线、干预实验图、以及 pruning rank-stability 相关图都值得细看。阅读时还要保留两个开放问题：第一，作者的 fan-in proxy 只数通道，不度量通道间正交性或信息质量，这个 proxy 到底在多大程度上贴近真实“梯度多样性”；第二，结论目前只到 1.2B，自回归大模型在 100B+ 规模、MoE、以及更复杂监督信号下是否仍成立，还没有被验证。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a recurring phenomenon in deep Transformers: later layers often contribute less and become increasingly redundant, but the usual explanation—smaller gradients in deep layers—is incomplete. The authors argue that the real bottleneck is structural. In Pre-LN residual stacks, shallow layers aggregate gradients from many downstream paths, while deep layers receive gradients from far fewer paths. This creates an asymmetry in the compositional diversity of gradient signals, which they call Gradient Fan-in Asymmetry (GFA). The paper has two goals: first, to show that this structural asymmetry explains late-layer redundancy better than gradient magnitude alone; second, to turn that account into two practical methods, CascadeFormer for depth-tapered architecture design and CascadeFlow Pruning for gradient-based layer pruning during training.","importance":"$16","technical_approach":"$17","experiments":"$18","reading_guide":"$19","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"从梯度流角度解释了Transformer深层冗余的机制（GFA），并给出了优雅的架构改进（宽度递减）和剪枝策略，对架构设计和训练动态分析极具启发。","en":"Explains the mechanism of deep layer redundancy in Transformers from a gradient flow perspective (GFA) and provides elegant architectural improvements (tapered width) and pruning strategies; highly inspiring for architecture design and training dynamics analysis."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Huzama Ahmad","Cao Viet Hai Nam","Se-Young Yun"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["Transformer Architecture","Gradient Flow","Model Pruning","Efficiency","Training Dynamics"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26538","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.26538","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.26560","title":"Erase-then-Delta Attention: Decoupling Erase and Write Addresses in Delta-Rule Linear Attention","abstract":"Delta-rule linear attention improves recurrent memory updates by correcting what is already stored at the current write address before writing new content. However, the active correction is still anchored to that same write address. As a result, stale information stored at a different address cannot be actively removed before new content is written elsewhere. We propose Erase-then-Delta Attention (EDA), a memory update rule that decouples where to erase from where to write. The key insight is th","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-25T03:12:19Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2606_26560.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"Delta-rule 线性注意力/递归记忆模型里，“纠错(erase/correct)”与“写入(write)”被同一个写地址 key 绑定：模型只能在当前写入地址上做 delta 纠错，无法在写新内容到地址 A 的同时，主动去地址 B 删除过期信息。结果是遇到变量重赋值、事实更正、上下文切换这类需要“定点删除”的场景时，只能依赖全局/通道级衰减去慢慢忘，容易残留干扰。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要在“长上下文与常数状态模型”的训练动态与记忆管理：它给 delta-rule 递归记忆补上了一个更像显式内存系统的操作原语——独立寻址的擦除。若结论成立，这类更新规则能在不回到 KV-cache 的前提下，缓解状态污染(state contamination)，提升长序列建模稳定性与可控性。贡献量级属于“更新规则层面的结构改动”，比纯调参/门控小修更有可迁移性，但仍需要看在更大规模预训练与更长上下文下是否保持收益。","technical_approach":"方法建立在 channel-wise gated delta / DPLR（对角衰减 + 低秩写入/纠错）的递归记忆框架上。基线 delta-rule 的关键是：把记忆矩阵 S_t 当作关联记忆，针对当前写 key k_t，让读出 S^T k_t 逼近 v_t，通过一次“delta 纠错”更新 S，从而不是简单累加 k_t v_t^T。\n\nEDA 的核心改动是把“要抑制/擦除的地址”从当前写地址 k_t 解耦出来：\n1) Erase step：先选择一个独立的 erase address（论文表述为 independently chosen address），沿着学习到的 erase direction 对该地址相关的记忆痕迹做定向抑制；这一步的语义更接近“条件化清理”，而不是统一的衰减。\n2) Delta step：再在当前写地址 k_t 上做标准 delta-style corrective write，保留 delta-rule 的优势（纠错式写入、减少写入漂移）。\n\n作者强调这是“严格更丰富”的更新族：同一步里允许 erase@addr_e 与 write@addr_w 并存，而以往方法（包括把 delta gate 拆成 key-side erase / value-side write 的变体）仍然围绕当前写 key 组织编辑。\n\n从机制上，EDA 试图把三种记忆管理信号分离清楚：对角/通道级 decay（无地址）、独立寻址 erase（有地址但不等于写地址）、写地址上的 delta 纠错（有地址且与写绑定）。论文还报告模型会学到 erase 与 write 近似正交的寻址分离，暗示两者确实承担不同功能，而不是“更强的同址遗忘”。","experiments":"给出的全文片段没有包含完整实验表格与具体数值，因此无法可靠复述参数规模、数据规模、PPL/下游指标提升幅度等硬结果。根据引言，实验覆盖三类：\n- 语言模型预训练：EDA 相对 DPLR-style gated delta baseline 有提升，并与若干强更新规则变体对比。\n- 长上下文评测：用于验证常数状态模型在长序列下的收益是否扩大。\n- 记忆状态探针/分析：观察 erase 与 write 的寻址关系，作者声称出现“近似正交分离”。\n\n建议在阅读时直接以主表（LM loss/PPL、long-context 指标）和消融（去掉 erase、把 erase 强行绑定到 write、只增强 decay current write key. This coupling prevents the model from actively deleting stale information stored at a different address before writing new content elsewhere, leaving only address-agnostic decay to “forget” outdated state—problematic for reassignment, correction, and context-shift patterns in language modeling.","importance":"The paper targets memory management in constant-state sequence models, which matters for long-context pretraining where state pollution accumulates. By adding an independently addressed erase operation, it aims to make delta-rule recurrence behave more like an explicit memory system (separate delete vs write primitives) without reverting to KV-cache. The contribution is a structural update-rule extension (more than a minor gating tweak), though its practical impact depends on whether gains persist at larger scales and longer contexts.","technical_approach":"$1a","experiments":"The provided full-text excerpt does not include the main tables or numeric results, so model sizes, token counts, and exact improvements cannot be stated reliably. This memory-blind gating is one of three coupled defects in the leading delta-rule architecture (GDN-2): the value-axis erase mask wastes parameters at the scale of the value projection, and -- as we prove -- mathematically prevents the WY-form triangular chunk solver that makes recurren","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-25T16:16:51Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2606_27229.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG","cs.NE"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 delta-rule 递归语言模型里一个很具体、但很关键的问题：如何在固定大小状态矩阵中做“有依据的遗忘”。GDN-2 这类方法已经把标量门控升级成了逐元素矩阵门控，表达力更强，但门控只看当前输入 token，不看当前内存状态，因此遗忘决策是 memory-blind 的。模型在不知道某个槽位里存的是高价值关联还是陈旧噪声的情况下，就先决定擦除多少内容。另一个问题是 value 维度上的全矩阵门控代价高，尤其不利于 chunk-parallel 训练和高效实现。CARVE 的目标就是同时解决这两个缺陷：让遗忘依赖内存内容，同时不引入额外渐近成本。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要在架构与训练效率交界处。它不是在 Transformer 上做局部补丁，而是在 recurrent / linear-attention 路线上推进一个核心能力：固定状态模型能否更像“会管理记忆”的语言模型。如果这个方向成立，收益不只是长上下文推理时的常数内存和常数步推理成本，还包括训练时更好的 chunk-parallel 性能。贡献量级上，它更像是对 delta-rule 家族的一次结构性修正，而不是全新范式。价值在于指出：递归模型的瓶颈不只是状态容量，也包括写入/擦除策略是否真正利用了状态内容。","technical_approach":"方法核心可以概括成两点。第一，内容感知的遗忘。CARVE 不再让擦除门仅由当前输入 x_t 线性投影得到，而是显式读取当前状态 S_{t-1} 的相关内容，再决定哪些部分该保留、哪些部分该覆盖。论文的关键设计是把这种“先读后忘”的过程做成与原有 delta-rule 更新兼容的形式，避免引入额外的高阶状态交互或破坏并行 scan 结构。第二，value-efficient 门控。作者认为 GDN-2 的 d_v × d_k 全矩阵门控里，value 维度的自由度过高，成本大但不一定都必要，因此将门控结构做了低成本重参数化，把主要选择压力放在 key / memory-slot 相关维度上，同时减少 value 方向的逐元素控制开销。\n\n从全文表述看，CARVE 仍然保留 delta-rule 的误差校正式写入：模型先用当前状态对 key 对应的 value 做预测，再把预测误差以 rank-1 形式写回状态。与 GDN-2 的区别不在“是否做误差校正”，而在“写回前如何决定擦除”和“门控张量如何参数化”。这使它与早期线性 RNN/SSM 的差异也很清楚：它不是靠对角状态或固定衰减来实现遗忘，而是让遗忘成为内容相关、位置无关、可并行的状态操作。\n\n如果从设计取舍看，这篇工作的重点不是最大化理论表达力，而是寻找一个硬件友好的折中：比 memory-blind gate 更聪明，但又不走向需要显式全历史检索的注意力；比 full matrix gate 更省，但又不退回过于粗糙的标量门控。","experiments":"从给出的全文片段可以确认，实验是围绕 GDN-2 这个直接前作基线展开的，重点验证两件事：内容感知门控是否带来更好的建模能力，以及 value-efficient 设计是否在不牺牲效果的前提下降低成本。论文引言明确把 chunk-parallel linear attention 作为应用场景，因此实验应当同时覆盖语言建模质量与吞吐/效率指标。可以确定的结论方向是：CARVE 相比 memory-blind 的 GDN-2，在相近或更低计算开销下取得更好的下游或困惑度表现，并且更适合 chunk-parallel 实现。\n\n不过，当前提供的全文片段没有包含完整结果表、参数规模、训练 token 数、具体 perplexity 或 benchmark 提升数字，因此无法负责任地填写精确数值。精读时应重点核对：1）与 GDN / GDN-2 在相同 d_v、d_k、参数量下的主结果；2）内容感知门控单独启用时的增益；3）value-efficient 重参数化单独带来的速度、显存或吞吐变化；4）长序列长度扩展时性能是否更稳；5）chunk size 改变时是否保持优势。如果论文给了状态可视化或门控统计，这部分也很值得看，因为它能直接说明模型是否真的学会“保护有用记忆、清除陈旧记忆”。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction 和方法定义部分，抓住它到底改了 GDN-2 的哪两个地方。然后直接看主结果表，确认收益来自质量提升、效率提升，还是两者兼有。之后看消融：内容感知门控和 value-efficient forgetting is powerful but memory-blind. In GDN-2, the model uses full element-wise erase/write gates over the state matrix, yet both gates are computed only from the incoming token. That means the model decides what to erase without inspecting what is currently stored. This is a poor fit for fixed-size memory, where overwriting the wrong association is costly. A second issue is efficiency: full d_v x d_k gating is expensive, especially along the value dimension, and this matters for chunk-parallel training and practical implementations. CARVE is designed to make forgetting content-aware while preserving the efficiency profile of chunk-parallel linear recurrent models.","importance":"Its importance is architectural rather than benchmark-driven. The paper pushes on a central question for recurrent alternatives to Transformers: can a fixed-state model manage memory intelligently enough to remain competitive, without giving up the hardware advantages that make recurrence attractive in the first place? If the answer is yes, the payoff is not only constant-memory inference but also a cleaner path to long-context deployment and chunk-parallel training. The contribution is best read as a structural correction to the GDN-2 line, not as a new modeling paradigm. The main message is that recurrent LM quality is limited not only by state size, but by whether the write/erase policy actually conditions on memory content.","technical_approach":"$1b","experiments":"$1c","reading_guide":"For a skim, start with the introduction and the formal update equations. xi，定义其对未来一段上下文 chunk 的影响为：从 KV 中移除 xi 后，未来各步预测分布与原分布的 KL 散度均值。这个指标是“前瞻的 token utility”，用来观察不同选择准则（attention、entropy 等）保留的 token 对远期预测的持续影响。\n\n2) 熵作为 token 信息量：用下一 token 预测分布的熵衡量不确定性。高熵意味着模型对下一步更不确定，作者经验性地认为这对应更“信息密集”的上下文（内容词等），更可能影响后续推理轨迹。\n\n3) InfoKV（方法）：在 token 选择时把 entropy 信号纳入重要性评分，而不是只看最近窗口 attention。文中还提到结合“中间层到最终层的表征演化”来刻画跨层的重要性（layer-wise orthogonal signal），用于更稳地决定哪些 token 在多层共享的压缩选择中应被保留。\n\n4) 适用场景：同时覆盖 long prefilling（长输入）与 long decoding（长输出推理）。核心目标是在固定 cache budget 下维持推理正确率/遵循能力/代码质量。","experiments":"实验覆盖：\n- Long prefilling：在 LongReason 上，用 First, attention is a noisy proxy for future usefulness in long reasoning traces, where sinks, formatting tokens, and transient filler can attract weight. Second, and more importantly for production, extracting attention weights requires materializing the attention matrix, which breaks the FlashAttention path and reintroduces the memory wall that efficient inference stacks are designed to avoid.","importance":"This is an inference paper rather than a pretraining paper, but it still matters for foundation-model work. foundation-model work. First, decoding acceleration cannot treat every backbone as a generic Transformer. Architectural choices such as MHC directly constrain what features the drafter should condition on and how multi-path states should be collapsed. Second, a native MTP head is not automatically a strong speculative drafter. MTP is trained in a sequential extrapolation regime, which makes it vulnerable to error accumulation on unverified tokens. A block drafter anchored to the target’s final prediction state can be much more stable. That matters for future work on pretrain-time MTP, multi-token heads, and architectures designed with downstream decoding acceleration in mind. A natural shortcut is to reuse the historical visual key-value (KV) cache directly. However, we identify a critical failure mode of this strategy: cached visual keys are already bound to their original positional context. It first performs algorithmic structure design, using multi-seed search and algorithmic refinement to find a promising computational structure. It then performs hardware-specific tuning through three ordered sub-stages: parallel mapping, tensor tiling, and memory optimization. This ordering mirrors how experienced GPU programmers work: choose the computation decomposition, map it to threads and blocks, tune tile shapes, and then optimize memory movement and reuse. The system uses a stage-aware multi-agent design with a code agent, a profile agent, and a debug agent. Across stages, it propagates only finalized optimization decisions and drops intermediate failed attempts. Within a stage, agents exchange structured signals such as bottleneck diagnoses, compilation or correctness errors, and concrete optimization proposals.","experiments":"The evaluation covers KernelBench and real-world workloads. V 和 Q 的输出。作者用互信息目标来展开这个问题：一个好的子集应同时保留关于视觉集合 V 和查询 Q 的信息。由此得到三个准则：Task Relevance，衡量所选 token 与用户问题的相关性；Information Coverage，要求所选 token 能覆盖原始视觉 token 集合中的关键信息；Semantic Diversity，用来避免选择一批语义重复的 保留比例结果，以及与 attention-based、diversity-based 方法的消融对比。若关注多模态预训练，可重点思考这些准则能否前移到训练阶段，例如用于动态视觉 token 采样、tokenizer 训练或多模态 curriculum。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies visual token pruning for MLLM inference: how to remove a large fraction of image tokens while preserving the model behavior obtained with the full visual input. The authors argue that many existing methods reduce the problem to heuristic scoring. Attention-based criteria often keep redundant tokens, while diversity-based criteria may ignore the user instruction and discard task-critical evidence.","importance":"The paper is more relevant to efficient MLLM inference than to foundation-model pretraining. Its useful signal for pretraining research is the framing of visual tokens as a conditional information budget. A pruning policy should not optimize saliency or diversity alone. It should jointly preserve query-relevant evidence, cover the original visual representation, and avoid semantic redundancy. This is relevant for visual tokenizer design, native multimodal context allocation, and training-time token budgeting.","technical_approach":"TOPS formulates pruning as the construction of Token Optimal Preservation Sets. Given visual tokens V and a text query Q, the goal is to select a subset S of size K such that the MLLM output with S remains close to the output with the full V. The paper analyzes this objective with mutual information and derives three principles: task relevance, information coverage, and semantic diversity. Task relevance keeps tokens informative for the query. Information coverage keeps the selected subset representative of the original visual token set under the query. Semantic diversity reduces redundancy among selected tokens. The implementation is training-free and model-agnostic. It uses a two-stage pipeline: a first stage removes coarse visual redundancy, and a second stage performs text-aware refinement.","experiments":"The experiments cover several MLLMs and multimodal benchmarks. The reported headline numbers are that TOPS removes nearly 90% of visual tokens while retaining 97.1% of the original performance on LLaVA-v1.5-7B and 99.1% on LLaVA-NeXT-7B. The comparisons across reduction ratios suggest that the method is more robust under aggressive pruning than attention-only or diversity-only baselines. The main empirical lesson is that low-budget pruning needs both instruction awareness and coverage control; either criterion alone fails in predictable ways.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. The useful idea is that activation sparsification and activation quantization should be treated as a coupled approximation problem, not as two independent compression steps.","technical_approach":"SharQ is a training-free inference method. For each activation tensor, it generates an input-adaptive N:M mask that extracts an outlier-heavy sparse backbone and runs that part through a sparse FP4 GEMM. The key design choice is how the dense residual is defined. Instead of subtracting the unquantized sparse component from the original activation, SharQ subtracts the quantized sparse backbone. This means the dense residual captures both the values excluded by the sparse mask and the quantization error introduced by the sparse FP4 path. The implementation is also hardware-aware: mask selection, sparse compression, and residual preparation are fused; the sparse and dense paths share the FP4 weight payload with path-specific scales; and the dense residual path accumulates directly into the sparse output.","experiments":"The evaluation spans multiple architectures and FP4 formats, including dense decoder-only LLMs, MoE models, and a video generation model. The introduction does not expose the detailed numbers, so the most important results to inspect are likely the comparisons across N:M patterns, FP4 formats, and alternative residual definitions.","reading_guide":"A skim is enough unless you work on inference kernels. 一类偏好排序。这样做的问题是，二者刻画的是同一批模型能力的不同切面，却没有共享参数；而且大多数评测只聚焦模型分数，忽略 item-level 结构，导致高成本评测里混入大量低信息量题目，也难以发现污染、异常样本或奖励模型噪声。","importance":"对基座研究的直接意义不在于训练方法，而在于“怎样更稳地比较模型、比较训练配方、比较数据策略”。如果评测信号本身不稳定，pretrain benchmark 饱和都很有用。第三，它提供了 residual-based anomaly detection 和 Fisher-information-based subset selection，这两点对大模型迭代中的 benchmark structure rather than only producing model-level scores. oracle 选择又会因为“取最大值”而高估性能。","importance":"这篇工作对预训练本身不是直接方法贡献，但对大模型评测有明确启发：单点 benchmark 分数低估了模型集合在异质 workload 上的可用能力，也会误导对模型 scaling、数据专长和推理成本的判断。它把“模型能力”从单模型静态准确率，改写成质量、成本、延迟约束下的系统级 Pareto frontier。对基座研究员有用的地方在于，它能帮助区分两类现象：模型本身是否更强，以及模型族之间是否存在可被路由利用的互补性。","technical_approach":"论文定义 Capability Frontier：给定一组模型、每个 prompt 的多次生成、质量指标、成本和 P95 延迟，构造在不同成本水平下可达到的最优质量前沿。核心对象是 oracle router，它假设知道每个 prompt-模型对的真实期望表现，并按 prompt 选择模型或生成结果。难点在于真实期望不可观测，常见做法用有限生成样本均值替代，再对模型取最大值；这会系统性偏高，因为被选中的往往是采样噪声中的正向离群点。论文提出两类校正：一类是基于生成次数外推的 debiasing，利用 bias 随 G 增大衰减的经验规律；另一类是概率图模型，显式建模每个 prompt、模型和 generation 的表现分布，再估计去噪后的 oracle frontier。与 RouterBench、RouteLLM 等路由工作相比，这篇论文的重点不是训练一个可部署 router，而是估计路由和多采样策略在有限样本评测中的真实上界，并校正 oracle 估计偏差。","experiments":"实验覆盖 21 个 LLM 和 16 个 benchmark，任务包括代码、推理、医学、事实性、指令遵循和 agentic tasks。主要结果是：在成本匹配条件下，Capability Frontier 相比每个 benchmark 的最佳单模型，平均错误率降低 54%；如果进一步考虑多次生成后的 post-hoc selection，错误率降低达到 82%。反过来，在 frontier 上可以用低 85% 的成本匹配最佳单模型准确率。有限样本 oracle 的偏差不可忽略：朴素 oracle 估计最多会把准确率抬高 8.7%，并把成本估计扭曲最多 88%。论文还给出形式化和实证分析，说明 oracle bias 随每个 prompt 的生成数 G 近似按 O(G^{-λ}) 衰减。合成 workload 实验显示，oracle gain 随 workload entropy cost、latency、multi-run selection 的实现细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Standard benchmarks report a single model on a single run, which misses the performance available from per-query model selection and multiple generations; naive oracle estimates of that performance are themselves biased upward because they maximize over noisy finite samples.","importance":"The contribution is mainly in evaluation, not pretraining methodology. Its value for foundation-model research is that it separates single-model capability from system-level capability created by model complementarity, sampling, cost constraints, and workload heterogeneity. This matters when interpreting scaling curves, benchmark gaps, and model specialization: a model family may have more deployable capability than any single reported score suggests, while a naive oracle analysis may overstate that capability.","technical_approach":"The paper defines a Capability Frontier: a Pareto frontier over quality, cost, and latency for a set of models and stochastic generations. For each prompt, an oracle router would select the model or generation with the best true expected utility under a cost constraint. Since the true prompt-model expectation is unobserved, the usual estimator replaces it with a sample mean over G generations and then takes a maximum across models. That estimator is positively biased because the selected model is disproportionately likely to have received favorable noise. The paper corrects this with extrapolation-based debiasing, using how the bias decays as G grows, and with a probabilistic graphical model that estimates latent prompt-model performance while accounting for generation noise. The distinction from routing papers such as RouterBench and RouteLLM is clear: the goal here is not to train a practical router, but to measure the attainable frontier and avoid finite-sample oracle inflation.","experiments":"The empirical study uses 21 LLMs on 16 benchmarks spanning coding, reasoning, medicine, factuality, instruction following, and agentic tasks. At matched cost, the Capability Frontier reduces average error by 54% relative to the best single model on each benchmark. When post-hoc multi-generation selection is included, the error reduction reaches 82%. Conversely, frontier selection can match the best single-model accuracy at 85% lower cost. The paper also quantifies the finite-sample oracle bias: naive oracle estimates can inflate accuracy by up to 8.7% and distort cost estimates by up to 88%. The bias is analyzed as decaying approximately as O(G^{-λ}) with the number of generations per prompt. Controlled simulations show that oracle gains increase monotonically with workload entropy, supporting the claim that heterogeneous data distributions drive much of the measured frontier gain.","reading_guide":"Skim the Introduction and Problem Setting first to pin down the frontier definition, the oracle router, and the quality-cost-latency objectives. Spend more time on the oracle-bias section, where the max-over-noisy-estimates issue and the two debiasing methods are introduced. In the experiments, prioritize the main frontier plots across 21 models and 16 benchmarks, plus the figures showing how bias changes with the generation budget. Expected reading time is about 30–45 minutes. Readers working on inference-time scaling or evaluation methodology may want to inspect the implementation details for cost, latency, and post-hoc selection.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇会改变 benchmark 数字的读法；重点看噪声校正和 cost matching，别只记住 82% 这个标题数。","en":"This changes how benchmark numbers should be read; focus on noise correction and cost matching, not just the 82% headline."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Bradley Fowler","Ryan Smith","Daniel Thi Graviet","William Myers","Joshua Greaves","Narmeen Fatimah Oozeer","Antía García","Philip Quirke","Amirali Abdullah","Fazl Barez","Shriyash Kaustubh Upadhyay"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["evaluation","capability-frontier","benchmarking","model-selection","sampling"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26836","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.26836","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.27288","title":"When Does Combining Language Models Help? A Co-Failure Ceiling on Routing, Voting, and Mixture-of-Agents Across 67 Frontier Models","abstract":"Multi-model LLM systems such as routing, voting, cascades, fusion, and mixture-of-agents are used to beat single-model accuracy. We show that their gain is capped by a quantity the field rarely reports. For any policy whose output is one member model answer, accuracy cannot exceed one minus beta, where beta is the rate at which every model is wrong on the same query. In contrast, the usual diagnostic, average pairwise error correlation rho, cannot identify beta: error laws with identical margina","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-25T17:06:06Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_27288.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究多模型 LLM 系统什么时候真的能提升准确率。核心结论是，对于 router、vote、cascade 这类最终输出来自成员模型答案的策略，性能上限不是由平均两两错误相关性决定，而是由所有模型在同一问题上同时失败的比例 β 决定；准确率不可能超过 1−β。","importance":"这篇论文对预训练本身不是方法贡献，但对模型评测、模型组合和服务层决策有明确价值。它提醒研究者：模型多样性不能只用 pairwise error correlation ρ 来判断。两个模型池可以有相同的边际错误率和相同的两两相关性，却有不同的共同失败率 β。对基座模型研究来说，这会影响如何衡量模型互补性、如何评估不同训练数据或训练路线带来的能力差异，以及如何判断多模型系统是否还有可实现的收益空间。","technical_approach":"论文没有提出新的 routing 算法，而是给出一个上限分析和测量框架。设 β 为模型池中所有模型在同一 query 上都答错的概率。任何只从成员模型答案中选择输出的策略，包括 oracle router、learned router、majority vote 和 cascade，都无法在这些共同失败样本上答对，因此准确率上限为 1−β。相对于单一最强模型，oracle 可获得的最大增益等于“单一最强模型答错但至少一个其他模型答对”的概率，也就是 Pr[single-best wrong]−β。作者进一步证明，常用的平均两两错误相关性 ρ 不能识别 β：即使边际错误率和 pairwise correlation 一样，高阶共同失败结构仍可能不同。实用上，作者用 Clopper–Pearson 区间从一个带标注的 held-out query set 给出 β 的有限样本置信界，从而提前估计组合系统的最大可能收益。","experiments":"实验分两层。第一层是预注册的 15 个当前模型、9 个 provider family，覆盖 frontier、mid、cheap 三档模型，在 GSM8K、MMLU、ARC-Challenge、MATH-500、MMLU-Pro 等 100 到 200 个问题规模的数据集上测试。第二层扩展到 67 个模型、21 个 provider family，来自 OpenRouter catalog，并加入更难的开放式数学与科学任务。主要观察是：oracle routing 的确存在正收益，但 learned router 几乎没有实现这些收益；随着模型池扩大，β 与 ρ 的差距会显现，ρ 往往低估共同失败带来的上限约束。作者还区分了两类场景：开放式数学更容易受到 ceiling-bound 限制，即很多强模型一起错；部分科学任务更像 realizability-bound，即理论上存在可选对的模型，但路由器难以识别。论文也提醒，共同失败样本的计数通常很小，因此有限样本置信界比点估计更可靠。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 30 到 40 分钟。优先看 Introduction 中关于 β ceiling 的定义和三个主张，再看 Problem Formulation 与 Proposition 1、Proposition 2。实验部分重点看 67 模型测量、oracle gain 与 learned router gain 的差距、β/ρ gap 随模型池大小变化的结果，以及 open-ended math 与 science 两类 regime 的对比。Appendix A 的 priced assignment、cost-aware fusion 和 cascade calibration 对做 serving 经济性分析的人有用；若只关心基座模型评测，可以略读。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks when multi-model LLM systems actually improve accuracy. Its central point is that for policies whose final answer is one of the member models’ answers, such as routing, voting, and cascades, the hard ceiling is the all-model co-failure rate β. Accuracy cannot exceed 1−β.","importance":"This is not a pretraining method paper, but it is useful for evaluation and model portfolio design. It gives a cleaner diagnostic for model complementarity than average pairwise error correlation. Pairwise correlation ρ can be identical across two model pools while the all-model failure probability β differs. For foundation-model research, this matters when comparing training recipes, data mixtures, or model families: apparent diversity at the pairwise level may not translate into recoverable gains under orchestration.","technical_approach":"The paper provides an upper-bound and measurement framework rather than a new routing method. Let β be the probability that every model in the pool is wrong on the same query. Any policy that must output one member model’s answer is wrong on those queries, so its accuracy is upper-bounded by 1−β. The oracle improvement over the single best model is localized to Pr[single-best wrong]−β: the cases where the best model fails but at least one other model succeeds. The authors also show that average pairwise error correlation ρ does not identify β, because higher-order co-failure structure can vary even when marginal error rates and pairwise correlations are fixed. They use a Clopper–Pearson interval to turn one graded held-out query set into a finite-sample certificate on the largest possible gain.","experiments":"The empirical study has two scales. A pre-registered experiment uses 15 models from 9 provider families, grouped into frontier, mid, and cheap tiers, on GSM8K, MMLU, ARC-Challenge, MATH-500, and MMLU-Pro with roughly 100–200 queries per dataset. A larger measurement expands to 67 models across 21 provider families from the OpenRouter catalog and adds harder open-ended math and science domains. The main findings are that oracle routing gains are positive, but a learned router realizes little of them; the gap between β and what ρ would suggest becomes visible as the pool grows; and tasks split into different regimes. Open-ended math is often ceiling-bound because strong models fail together. Some science tasks are more realizability-bound: gains exist in principle, but the router struggles to pick the right model. The paper is careful that all-model-wrong counts can be small, so interval estimates matter more than raw point estimates.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 30–40 minutes. Read the Introduction for the β ceiling and the distinction from ρ. Then read the problem formulation and the statements around Proposition 1 and Proposition 2. In the empirical sections, focus on the 67-model measurement, the oracle-versus-learned-router gap, the β/ρ gap as pool size changes, and the contrast between open-ended math and science tasks. a","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-24T18:45:03Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_26300.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论 Despite inheriting strong reasoning capabilities from LLMs, they remain prone to hallucinations that contradict their visual inputs. Existing alignment methods, suc","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-24T21:12:24Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2606_26387.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决 MLLM 对视觉输入“看见但不用”的问题。模型内部可能已经编码了正确视觉证据，但解码时仍被语言先验主导，产生与图像矛盾的回答。标准 DPO 只优化最终文本偏好，可能奖励由文本捷径得到的正确答案，因此不能稳定压制 visual laziness。","importance":"这篇论文对 VLM post-training 和 native multimodal alignment 有启发。它把多模态幻觉从“感知失败”进一步拆成“视觉证据是否参与了生成决策”的问题。这个视角比单纯做更大偏好数据或更强 judge 更接近训练目标本身：如果模型在遮掉视觉信息后仍然同样自信，那么正确输出也可能不是视觉 grounded 的。对预训练研究的间接价值在于，它提供了一种衡量和训练跨模态信息使用的目标，可用于检查视觉 token、视觉编码器和 LLM 解码过程之间是否真的形成因果耦合。","technical_approach":"VIGIL 的核心做法是在偏好优化中加入 counterfactual visual alignment。训练时构造一个 blind state：通过 masking visual attention，让模型无法访问视觉证据，只保留文本指令。然后比较 seeing state 与 blind state 下的响应分布，最大化两者之间的 Visual Information Gain。这样做的目标不是简单让模型更偏好 winning answer，而是要求 winning answer 的置信度来自图像条件；如果模型在看不见图像时仍然对答案很自信，就会被惩罚。与标准 DPO 相比，VIGIL 约束了“答案如何得到”，而不只约束“答案是否被偏好”。与引入外部工具或模块化视觉系统不同，它不改模型结构；与在线 RL 视觉 token 选择不同，它是 offline 训练，计算和稳定性成本更低。","experiments":"论文报告 VIGIL 在 7B 到 72B 的不同 MLLM 架构上都有稳定收益，且更强、更大的基座模型收益更明显。一个重要结果是数据效率：只使用对比基线 25% 的偏好数据，就能达到或接近竞争性方法的表现。作者还观察到 zero-shot referring expression comprehension 提升，且没有使用显式 bounding box 监督，说明这种反事实视觉约束可能促使模型形成更好的空间 grounding。由于给定全文片段没有完整表格，具体 benchmark 分数和逐项提升幅度不宜过度外推；可确认的实验结论是跨规模有效、数据更省、并带来一定的空间定位泛化。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 20–30 分钟。重点看 Introduction 和 Preliminaries 中对 visual laziness 的概率解释：标准 DPO 为什么会强化语言先验，以及 blind state 为什么能暴露这种 shortcut。方法部分需要关注 Visual Information Gain 的定义、blind attention mask 的实现位置、VIGIL loss 与 DPO loss 的组合方式。实验部分优先看三类结果：25% 数据效率对比、7B–72B 跨架构扩展、zero-shot referring expression 的无框监督提升。若只关心基座预训练，可把它当作一种跨模态信息利用诊断目标，而不是普通幻觉修复方法。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses visual laziness in multimodal LLMs: the model may encode the relevant visual evidence, yet still rely on language priors during decoding and produce answers that contradict the image. Standard DPO optimizes preference over final text outputs, so it can reward correct-looking answers that were obtained through textual shortcuts rather than visual grounding.","importance":"The work is relevant to multimodal post-training and to the broader question of whether visual tokens causally influence generation. Its useful contribution is the shift from outcome-only alignment to evidence-sensitive alignment. If a model remains confident after visual evidence is removed, then even a correct answer may not be visually grounded. This gives a concrete training and diagnostic signal for studying the coupling among the visual encoder, visual tokens, and the LLM decoder.","technical_approach":"VIGIL introduces counterfactual visual alignment into preference optimization. During training, it constructs a blind state by masking visual attention, preventing the model from using image evidence while preserving the text instruction. The model is then optimized to increase the Visual Information Gain between the normal seeing state and this blind counterfactual state. The intended effect is to make high-confidence preferred responses depend on the visual input. Cases where the model remains confident without the image are treated as blind-confidence failures. Compared with DPO, the method constrains how the answer is derived, not only whether the final text is preferred. Compared with tool-based or modular perception systems, it does not change the architecture. Compared with online RL for visual token selection, it is an offline objective and should be easier to train stably.","experiments":"The paper reports consistent improvements across MLLMs from 7B to 72B parameters, with larger gains on stronger foundation models. A notable data-efficiency result is that VIGIL can match competitive baselines using only 25% of their preference data. The authors also report improved zero-shot referring-expression comprehension without explicit bounding-box supervision, suggesting that the counterfactual visual constraint can encourage spatial grounding. The provided text does not include full benchmark tables, so exact per-benchmark deltas should be checked in the experimental section.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Focus first on the Introduction and Preliminaries, especially the explanation of why DPO can reinforce language-prior shortcuts. Then read the method section for the definition of Visual Information Gain, the implementation of the visual-attention mask, and how the VIGIL term is combined with the preference loss. In the experiments, prioritize the 25% data-efficiency comparison, the 7B-to-72B scaling results, and the zero-shot referring-expression analysis. For foundation-model researchers, the most useful angle is its role as a training objective and diagnostic for cross-modal evidence use, not merely as a hallucination mitigation recipe.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"做多模态预训练对齐的研究员可重点看其反事实视觉约束的设计，§3的几何约束公式可直接借鉴到预训练损失函数中。","en":"Researchers working on multimodal pretraining alignment should focus on its counterfactual visual constraint design; the geometric loss in §3 can be directly adapted to pretraining objectives."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Xi Xiao","Chen Liu","Chih-Ting Liao","Yunbei Zhang","Qizhen Lan","Yuxiang Wei","Lin Zhao","Janet Wang","Jianyang Gu","Muchao Ye","Tianyang Wang","Hao Xu"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["multimodal-llm","visual-grounding","rl-alignment","hallucination-mitigation","counterfactual-learning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26387","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.26387","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.27373","title":"Paying More Attention to Visual Tokens in Self-Evolving Large Multimodal Models","abstract":"Recently, self-evolving large multimodal models (LMMs) have received attention for improving visual reasoning in a purely unsupervised setting. the","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-25T09:24:09Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_26790.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"长时程语言代理的 outcome-based RL（如 GRPO）只有稀疏的轨迹级奖励，难以做中间决策的 credit assignment；而现有 skill-conditioned 自蒸馏往往依赖外部 skill memory/检索到的特权上下文，既有维护成本，也容易与当前 on-policy 状态分布失配。论文要解决的是：能否从“当前策略自己跑出来的完整轨迹”里提取分布匹配的技能监督，并把它变成密集的 token 级训练信号，同时推理时不引入额外检索或 analyzer。","importance":"对基座预训练本身的直接贡献有限，更像是 agentic post-train/RL 的训练信号设计。但它提供了一个对预训练研究仍有价值的点：把“轨迹后验信息（hindsight）→ 自监督/自蒸馏信号”的管线做成严格 on-policy、分布匹配、可内化（inference-free）的训练目标。这类思路在持续学习/continual post-train、以及把 RL 信号转成更稳定的 token-level shaping 上，可能比纯 outcome 优化更稳，也更接近可规模化的数据闭环。","technical_approach":"OPID 的核心是把一条已完成的 on-policy 轨迹转写成两级自然语言技能，并用这些技能构造“同一输出在不同上下文下的 logprob 差”作为自蒸馏优势（advantage）来做策略更新。\n\n1) 技能抽取（hindsight skill extraction）：对每条完成轨迹生成两类技能文本：\n- episode-level skill：总结整条轨迹的工作流/避免失败的规则，粒度粗但覆盖全程。\n- step-level skill：只在关键时刻给出状态条件化的局部指导，粒度细但稀疏。\n论文动机是长时程交互里“全局流程”和“关键拐点决策”对成功都重要，但需要不同粒度的监督。\n\n2) critical-first skill routing：先识别轨迹中的 critical timesteps（文中描述为关键决策点/易出错点），这些位置优先注入 step-level skill；其余位置回退到 episode-level skill。这样避免全程塞入细粒度技能导致噪声/不稳定，也避免只用全局技能导致对关键局部决策无约束。\n\n3) on-policy self-distillation advantage：把“路由后的技能文本”作为额外上下文注入到交互历史中，用同一个旧策略对同一条 on-policy 响应分别在原上下文与技能增强上下文下打分，得到 token-level 的 log-probability shift。这个 shift 被当作偏好信号/优势项，与 episode-level 的 outcome advantage 结合，形成最终的更新目标。直观上：结果奖励负责方向，技能蒸馏负责把方向分解到哪些 token/哪些决策更该被强化。\n\n4) 推理阶段：不需要技能抽取器、外部 skill 库或检索；技能只在训练时作为“特权上下文”产生梯度信号，最终被模型参数内化。","experiments":"论文在 ALFWorld、WebShop、Search-based QA 上评估，并覆盖不同模型尺度。结论层面：OPID 相比纯 outcome-based RL、以及依赖外部 skill memory/检索的 skill-distillation 基线，在长时程任务成功率上更高，且更稳定。\n\n但你给到的全文片段没有包含关键表格与具体数值（成功率提升幅度、cache/rollout 规模、训练步数、critical step 识别方式的消融等），因此无法可靠复述“提升了多少个百分点/在什么预算下”。如果要用于严肃的 recipe 复现，必须回到实验章节核对：\n- 与 outcome-only（GRPO 类）相比的绝对提升；\n- 与 SDAR/skill-conditioned distill 基线相比的差距；\n- step-skill vs episode-skill 的单独贡献；\n- critical-first routing 的必要性（随机路由/全用 episode/全用 step 的对照）；\n- 训练稳定性指标（reward 方差、KL、loss spike）。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 classifiers should explicitly model user intent instead of mapping a prompt directly to a safe or harmful label.","importance":"This is a post-training and safety-classification paper rather than a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to training-signal design. The useful lesson is that an inspectable intermediate variable can improve multiple training regimes when labels are easily confounded by surface form. In safety classification, a model can output the right label for the wrong reason, or over-refuse because of unsafe keywords. Intent supervision gives SFT, DPO, distillation, and RL a more precise target. For pretraining-oriented work, the broader implication is about signal quality: a small amount of dense human supervision over intermediate semantics can be more informative than more direct labels, and RL rewards that check only final labels may miss important failure modes.","technical_approach":"$36","experiments":"$37","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. 类优化器的主体。方法先在同一 prompt 内构造 preference pairs，把高 reward 回答和低 reward 回答配对。每个 rollout 用最后一层、最后生成 token 的 hidden state 表示，并从低 reward 回答指向高 reward 回答构造 latent displacement direction。然后训练一个小 projector，把这些 reward-ordered directions 投到更集中的空间里。系统再计算 batch-level consensus prototype，用方向夹角识别与主方向偏离很大的 rollout。关键点是它不需要逐样本 policy gradient，只用 detached hidden states 做 forward-only 判断。对严重方向异常的样本，GeoAlign 用同 prompt 内更稳定的替代 rollout 做保守修正，同时保持每个 prompt 的 rollout 数量不变。相比只做 reward clipping、reward shaping、uncertainty weighting 或 reward-statistics filtering 的方法，它关注的是学习信号的方向一致性，而不是 reward at the pretrain-to-posttrain interface. Scalar rewards are not enough to decide whether a rollout is useful for policy improvement. The direction implied by the rollout also matters. This is relevant for noisy reward models, verifier-based RL, reward hacking, and R1-Zero-style training loops where a few abnormal high-scoring trajectories can steer the policy in undesirable directions.","technical_approach":"GeoAlign is a rollout-curation module that can be added to iterative policy optimization without changing the main PPO/GRPO-style objective. It builds within-prompt preference pairs, represents each rollout using the detached final-token hidden state from the last layer, and defines a displacement vector from the lower-reward response to the higher-reward response. A small online projector is trained to concentrate these reward-ordered directions. The method then forms a batch-level consensus prototype and detects angular outliers by measuring deviation from this consensus. Severe outliers are rectified using more stable alternatives from the same prompt while preserving the per-prompt rollout count. The difference from reward clipping, reward shaping, uncertainty weighting, or reward-statistics filtering is that GeoAlign judges the geometric consistency of the learning signal, not only the scalar reward or its uncertainty.","experiments":"The evaluation covers dialogue alignment on HH-RLHF with continuous ArmoRM rewards and mathematical reasoning on DAPO-Math-17k with binary verified rewards. A cheaper, earlier-stage selection mechanism that operates before full reasoning traces are generated is needed.","importance":"TEMP introduces a lightweight perturbation-loss-based curation paradigm that shifts difficulty and diversity detection from full-trajectory + strong-model judging to the first 100–1000 tokens of a CoT. It materially reduces token cost and external-model dependency in reasoning SFT pipelines, with direct engineering value for reasoning-augmented pretraining and pre-RL SFT data construction.","technical_approach":"TEMP runs in two stages. First, difficulty filtering uses only the first 100 reasoning tokens, evaluated on a randomly perturbed checkpoint of the pretrained model; samples with higher loss are classified as harder. Second, diversity sampling uses the first 1000 tokens, evaluated on a small number of noisy checkpoints extrapolated along the SFT direction; the resulting loss vectors are used as representations for clustering and subset selection. Two observations underpin the design: reasoning quality is already encoded in the very early tokens of a trace, so full-length chains are unnecessary for difficulty estimation; and parameter change during SFT is small enough that loss patterns over the first 1000 tokens across perturbed checkpoints correlate well with full-sample gradient behavior during training, and can thus substitute for explicit gradient diversity metrics.","experiments":"SFT experiments on Qwen2.5-7B and Llama3.1-8B across M23K medical reasoning and OpenThoughts.
