We introduce a \\textbf{R}anking-\\textbf{i}nduced \\textbf{VER}ifiable framework (RiVER) that trains LLMs on score-based optimization tasks without ground-truth solutions, using deterministic execution feedback as continuous-valued supervision. When applying group-relative RL to such conti","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-25T17:59:36Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_27369.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"主流 RLVR（可验证奖励强化学习）依赖标准答案或二元对错判定，无法直接用于没有唯一真值、但可以执行并按目标函数打分的开放优化任务，例如算法竞赛中的启发式求解、规划、近似优化问题。","importance":"把 RLVR 的适用范围从「有标准答案的任务」扩展到「只有可执行目标函数的开放优化任务」，证明了无真值的排序式可验证反馈也能提升 LLM 的通用推理与代码能力，为 RL 预训练 / 后训练提供了一类全新的、可无限扩展的监督来源。","technical_approach":"提出 RiVER（Ranking-induced VERifiable）框架，核心是两个针对连续分数奖励的修正：一是按实例做组内排序（instance-wise ranking），每个测试实例只在同组采样的候选解之间比较相对优劣，消除不同实例之间分数量级差异导致的 scale dominance（分数范围大的实例主导梯度）；二是胜者加权的奖励塑形（winner-heavy reward shaping），拉大组内最优解与其余可行解的奖励差距，同时保留非胜者的有界连续反馈，抑制 frequency dominance（大量重复的次优解在梯度上压过少量高质量解）。训练信号完全来自候选解在隐藏实例上的执行结果，不需要参考程序、最优解或人工标注。","experiments":"在 AtCoder Heuristic Contest 这类无真值的开放优化任务上训练，评估同时覆盖分数型算法工程任务与传统二元通过/失败的代码基准（LiveCodeBench、USACO）。仅用开放优化任务训练后，RiVER 不仅在分数型任务上提升评分，还能稳定迁移到 LiveCodeBench 与 USACO；直接优化原始执行分数的基线虽然在分数型任务上有提升，但无法一致迁移到二元正确性基准，说明收益并非单纯来自更多可执行反馈，而是来自奖励校准本身。","reading_guide":"泛读为主，预计 25~35 分钟。重点关注引言中对 scale dominance 与 frequency dominance 的定义与例子，以及第 3 节 RiVER 两个设计选择的形式化与直觉；实验部分重点看迁移到 LiveCodeBench/USACO 的结果与对照基线的差异；可以跳过具体 AtCoder 任务的细节描述。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Dominant RLVR formulations rely on ground-truth answers or binary correctness criteria, and therefore cannot be directly applied to open optimization tasks that have no unique solution but are still verifiable via execution and an objective function — such as heuristic algorithm design, planning, and approximate optimization in programming contests.","importance":"RiVER extends RLVR beyond tasks with known ground truth to open optimization tasks that only expose an executable objective. It demonstrates that ground-truth-free, ranking-based verifiable feedback can improve both general reasoning and coding ability, opening a new, potentially unbounded source of supervision for RL pretraining and post-training.","technical_approach":"RiVER addresses two failure modes of naively applying continuous objective scores to group-relative policy gradients. First, instance-wise ranking compares candidate solvers only against other candidates evaluated on the same hidden test instance, removing cross-instance score-scale effects that cause scale dominance, where updates are driven by arbitrary numerical ranges rather than real quality gains. Second, winner-heavy reward shaping widens the reward gap between the best candidate in a rollout group and the rest, while still giving bounded non-binary feedback to valid non-winners. This mitigates frequency dominance, in which repeated suboptimal modes collectively contribute more gradient mass than rare but stronger solutions. All supervision comes from executing candidates on hidden instances; no reference programs, optimal solutions, or human labels are required.","experiments":"Training is conducted exclusively on ground-truth-free open optimization tasks from AtCoder Heuristic Contests. Evaluation covers both score-based algorithm engineering tasks and conventional pass/fail coding benchmarks (LiveCodeBench, USACO). RiVER improves ratings on score-based tasks and consistently transfers to LiveCodeBench and USACO. Baselines that directly optimize raw execution scores improve score-based ratings but fail to transfer consistently to exact-solution benchmarks, showing that the gain comes from reward calibration rather than simply exposing the model to more executable feedback.","reading_guide":"Skim-level read, ~25–35 minutes. Focus on the Introduction for precise definitions and motivating examples of scale dominance and frequency dominance, and on Section 3 for the formalization and intuition behind the two design choices. In experiments, prioritize the transfer results to LiveCodeBench / USACO and the comparison against raw-score baselines. Detailed descriptions of individual AtCoder tasks can be skipped.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看 reward shaping 细节；它把 RLVR 从答案判定推到优化得分，但 AtCoder heuristic 设定仍偏窄，先读方法和失败模式。","en":"Read the reward-shaping section; it pushes RLVR beyond exact answers, but the AtCoder-heuristic setup is still narrow, so treat the gains as directional."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Microsoft"],"authors":["Yingyu Lin","Qiyue Gao","Nikki Lijing Kuang","Xunpeng Huang","Kun Zhou","Tongtong Liang","Zhewei Yao","Yi-An Ma","Yuxiong He"],"notable_authors":["Yuxiong He","Zhewei Yao","Yi-An Ma"],"tags":["RLVR","reward-shaping","post-training","optimization-tasks","verifiable-rewards"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27369","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.27369","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.26997","title":"RolloutPipe: Overlapping Pipelined Rollout and Training in Disaggregated On-Policy LLM Reinforcement Learning","abstract":"Large language model (LLM) post-training for reasoning increasingly relies on reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), where models learn from ground-truth feedback on mathematical, logical, and scientific tasks. However, existing synchronous on-policy GRPO (Group Re","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-25T13:14:14Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_26997.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.DC","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"解耦式 on-policy GRPO 强化学习后训练中，rollout 生成与策略训练串行执行，导致训练 GPU 池在整个 rollout 阶段持续空闲；异步流水线虽能重叠两阶段，但会引入旧策略数据，破坏 on-policy 正确性。","importance":"揭示了 GRPO 组级依赖与串行执行范式之间的结构性矛盾，提出无需牺牲 on-policy 正确性的流水线重叠方案，直接降低 RLVR 后训练的端到端时间成本，对规模化 RL 后训练系统设计有直接参考价值。","technical_approach":"提出 RolloutPipe，基于 Slime 框架改造，核心有两个机制：1) complete-group pipelining (CGP)：以完整组为边界构建流水线，一旦某个 prompt 对应的 K 个响应全部生成并完成奖励计算、组统计、优势赋值（即组物化完成），立即送入训练侧 FIFO，无需等待全部 R 个组完成；当前轮次 rollout 权重保持固定，因此训练数据仍对应当前策略，不破坏 on-policy 性质。2) frontier-group dispatch (FGD)：rollout 节点的请求准入策略，优先调度构成下一个训练 batch 的 F_w 个前沿组，使可训练组更早、更稳定地到达训练端。","experiments":"在 Qwen3-1.7B 上进行端到端实验，覆盖 4 类推理与科学任务共 12 种配置。相比原生 Slime：rollout 开始到训练结束的主流程时间缩短 30.7%–42.3%，训练端等待比率下降 37%–76%。样例场景（R=96, K=8, LSAT-AR）中，首个组在 200s 即完成物化，原生 Slime 需等到 400s 全部组完成才提交训练，RolloutPipe 直接回收这 200s 的交错完成窗口空闲。","reading_guide":"泛读为主，约 25 分钟。重点看 §1 动机部分对 GRPO 组级依赖与 PPO 样本级依赖的结构性差异分析，以及图 1 的串行/流水线时序对比；§2 的两个核心机制 CGP 与 FGD 的设计细节；实验部分关注不同任务和 R/K 配置下加速比的波动规律，理解 rollout 资源与训练资源比例对收益的影响。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In disaggregated on-policy GRPO reinforcement learning post-training, rollout generation and policy training execute serially, leaving the trainer GPU pool idle for the entire rollout phase. Asynchronous pipelines overlap the two stages but introduce stale policy data, breaking on-policy correctness.","importance":"Identifies the structural mismatch between GRPO's group-level dependency and the serial execution paradigm, and proposes a pipeline overlapping scheme that preserves on-policy correctness. The approach directly reduces end-to-end wall-clock time of RLVR post-training and provides concrete system design insights for scalable RL post-training stacks.","technical_approach":"RolloutPipe is built on top of the Slime framework with two core mechanisms. First, complete-group pipelining (CGP): constructs a pipeline at complete-group boundaries. As soon as the K responses for a prompt finish generation, reward computation, group-statistics calculation, and advantage assignment (i.e., group materialization), the complete group is pushed into the trainer FIFO immediately, without waiting for all R groups in the round. Rollout weights for the current round stay fixed, so training data still matches the current policy and on-policy property is preserved. Second, frontier-group dispatch (FGD): a rollout-node admission policy that prioritizes requests for the F_w frontier groups forming the next trainer batch, so that trainable groups arrive earlier and more steadily.","experiments":"End-to-end experiments on Qwen3-1.7B across four reasoning and scientific workloads, covering 12 settings. different reasoning paths and different answers. The proposed explanation is that intermediate representations may be relatively language-agnostic, while the final generation stage becomes language- and script-specific when the model commits to discrete output tokens.","importance":"The main relevance is the modeling interface between hidden states and discrete tokens. SOLAR uses the expected embedding under the next-token distribution as an alignment target. This representation is closer to the language model’s output decision than a generic hidden state, but less tied to a particular token identity or writing system than the generated text. The idea is useful beyond multilingual SFT: when aligning reasoning traces across languages, tokenizers, or modalities, the soft decision distribution can be a meaningful training signal instead of aligning only hidden states or final strings.","technical_approach":"SOLAR is an auxiliary objective used during supervised fine-tuning. For parallel reasoning traces in English and non-English languages, the model computes the next-token distribution at each autoregressive position. That distribution is mapped into the embedding space by taking a probability-weighted expectation over the vocabulary embedding matrix, producing a soft-token representation. The representations are mean-pooled over positions to obtain one continuous vector for the trace. English is used as the pivot, and SOLAR minimizes cosine distance between the English vector and each non-English counterpart while retaining the standard SFT loss. This differs from inference-time soft thinking, which feeds soft tokens back during decoding; SOLAR instead uses soft tokens as a training-time cross-lingual alignment signal. It also avoids requiring token-level correspondence across scripts, which is fragile for languages such as Chinese, Thai, Japanese, and Telugu.","experiments":"The experiments use Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B across multilingual reasoning benchmarks, comparing regular SFT, inference-time soft thinking, and SOLAR. For Qwen3-4B, SOLAR improves accuracy by up to +3.8 over SFT and cross-lingual consistency by up to +4.5 on AIME 2024, with larger gains on lower-resource languages such as Swahili. Representation analysis shows that soft tokens carry a stronger final-layer cross-lingual signal than discrete tokens in base models, and that SOLAR produces stronger final-layer alignment than SFT or inference-time soft thinking alone. The behavioral checks are also important: SOLAR does not simply force English reasoning. On MGSM, SOLAR-tuned Qwen3-4B reasons in the target script 98.13% of the time, close to the base model’s 98.81%; SOLAR-tuned Qwen3-8B reaches 98.16%. This also fixes a base 8B failure mode where reasoning collapses to English for several non-Chinese languages, with Ja/Th/Te below 5% and a four-language average of 27.43%.","reading_guide":"A 25–35 minute skim is appropriate. In the experiments, focus on AIME 2024 and MGSM because they separate accuracy, cross-lingual consistency, and preservation of target-language reasoning. The representation analysis is the most informative part for modeling researchers, especially the comparison between soft-token and discrete-token alignment at the final layer. However, those methods tend to underperform when applied to large language models. We argue that such underperformance can be partly explained by the ``polysemantic'' nature of large language models: per-weight importance estimates utilized by EWC-style regularization are too coarse and cannot isolate the knowledge that needs protection. In th","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-25T05:46:04Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_26629.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作处理 LLM 持续学习中的遗忘问题，重点指出 EWC 等权重空间正则在 LLM 上效果弱，原因可能不是单纯调参不足，而是权重和神经元过于多义，不能作为选择性保护知识的合适单位。","importance":"对 continual pretrain 和部署后持续适配有一定参考价值。贡献不在于提出新的预训练目标，而在于把稳定性约束从 weight space 移到 SAE feature space。这个方向把 mechanistic interpretability 中的稀疏特征字典用于训练约束，给出一个更细粒度的知识保护坐标系。它也提示持续学习中的关键问题不只是正则强度，而是约束施加在哪个表示空间。对于不希望保存旧数据、旧 activation 或增加推理路由的场景，这类方法有现实意义。","technical_approach":"方法使用预训练 Sparse Autoencoder 作为冻结的特征字典。SAE 将模型 dense activation 映射到稀疏、过完备的 feature 表示，这些 feature 通常比单个神经元或单个权重更接近语义选择性。每个任务的数据先经过模型和 SAE，估计哪些 SAE feature 与当前任务相关，并形成 relevance mask。高相关 feature 被视为需要适配的区域，低相关或需保护的 feature 被纳入稳定性约束。训练目标来自一个稳定性-可塑性的约束优化形式，而不是手工加入一个权重漂移惩罚。稳定性约束要求受保护 feature 的漂移不超过预算；可塑性约束要求任务相关 feature 发生足够适配，避免模型通过冻结一切来降低遗忘。拉格朗日松弛和 squared-hinge penalty 产生两个损失：protect loss 和 guide loss。EWC 可以看作这个框架的退化形式：它在权重空间度量漂移，并且没有显式可塑性约束。方法在 mask 构建后只保存紧凑的 SAE feature mask，不保留旧任务样本、旧 activation，也不引入推理期路由。","experiments":"实验覆盖 TRACE-5000 和 MedCL，分别对应跨域和域内持续学习。结果显示，SAE-guided activation regularization 在 TRACE 上优于同设定下的权重空间正则、梯度投影和部分 replay baseline；在 MedCL 上，在不保存旧任务样本的方法中表现靠前。消融和机制分析强调两点：第一，SAE feature space 中的任务相关表示比 weight space 更容易分离；第二，EWC 的低遗忘往往来自过度压制可塑性，而不是精确保护旧知识。文中还与 SAE-FD 这类 anchor-based feature distillation 区分开来：后者依赖旧任务 anchor 或 activation matching，而该方法试图只用 feature mask 和当前任务数据完成正则。","reading_guide":"泛读约 20–30 分钟。优先看方法部分的三块内容：SAE feature mask 如何构建，protect loss / guide loss 如何从约束优化推出，以及 EWC 为什么是特例。实验部分先看 TRACE-5000 和 MedCL 的主表，再看与 EWC、replay、gradient projection、SAE-FD 的对比。消融建议重点看正则强度、稳定性预算、可塑性预算、SAE feature 选择阈值和层位置。如果只关心预训练侧启发，可以重点读机制分析：它说明持续学习正则的有效性依赖表示坐标系，而不只是 penalty coefficient。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The work addresses catastrophic forgetting in LLM continual learning. Its central claim is that weight-space regularizers such as EWC are poorly matched to LLMs because individual weights participate in many entangled features. Protecting a weight is therefore a blunt intervention: it can preserve one concept while unnecessarily constraining unrelated ones.","importance":"The contribution is most relevant to continual pretraining and post-deployment adaptation rather than initial base-model training. It proposes a different unit of preservation: SAE features instead of parameters. This is a useful bridge between mechanistic interpretability and training methodology. The main lesson is that continual-learning regularization depends on the coordinate system in which drift is measured. For settings where old data, stored activations, task-specific adapters, or inference-time routing are undesirable, feature-space constraints are a plausible design point.","technical_approach":"The method uses a pretrained sparse autoencoder as a frozen feature dictionary over model activations. Task data are encoded into sparse SAE features, and a relevance mask is constructed to identify which features are task-related. The training objective is derived from a constrained optimization view of stability and plasticity. Protected features should not drift beyond a stability budget, while task-relevant features should adapt enough to avoid the degenerate solution of preserving everything and learning little. A Lagrangian relaxation with squared-hinge penalties yields a protect loss and a guide loss. Under this formulation, EWC is a special case that measures drift in weight space and lacks an explicit plasticity constraint. After mask construction, the method keeps compact feature masks rather than previous-task examples, anchor activations, per-task parameters, or inference-time routers.","experiments":"The evaluation uses TRACE-5000 and MedCL, covering cross-domain and within-domain continual learning. Under matched training conditions, the SAE-guided method outperforms weight-space regularizers, gradient-projection baselines, and several replay-style alternatives on TRACE. On MedCL, it is among the stronger methods that do not retain previous-task examples. The mechanistic analyses support the motivation: task-relevant structure is more separable in SAE feature space than in weight space, and EWC tends to reduce forgetting by suppressing plasticity broadly rather than by selectively preserving old knowledge. The comparison with SAE-FD is also important: SAE-FD relies on stored anchors or activations, while this method aims to operate with masks and current-task data.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Read the method sections on mask construction, the constrained optimization derivation, and the protect/guide losses. Then inspect the TRACE-5000 and MedCL main tables, followed by comparisons against EWC, replay, gradient projection, and SAE-FD. agentic 行为可以在残差流中被较紧凑地定位。对预训练研究的启发有两点。第一，pretrain 与 post-train 的接口不只是数据和 loss，也包括可被定位、可被调制的内部方向。第二，能力差异可能不是均匀分布在全模型参数中，而是部分集中在稀疏特征和特定残差方向上。这会影响后续的持续训练、能力保留、模型合并、RL 安全开关和 runtime steering <tool_call> 调用的 RL 微调。模型维度 d=2048，共 36 层。作者训练 Dedicated Feature Crosscoder，将两个模型的 residual stream post-MLP 激活共同编码到稀疏字典中，再分别解码回两个模型。DFC 的字典被显式切成 A-exclusive、B-exclusive 和 shared 三块，并通过 gradient masking exclusive penalty λ_excl。技术重点有三处。第一，用 encode-decode reconstruction 测试 crosscoder 是否保留甚至转移工具调用能力。第二，用 Cohen’s d 按工具样本与通用文本激活差异筛选工具相关特征，并要求工具样本 firing rate ≥0.3、通用样本 firing rate ≤0.1。第三，对 A-exclusive 特征做 residual steering：把选中特征的 decoder column 乘以系数 α，加到 RL 模型 residual stream 中，观察工具调用行为是否可控。论文还用 mass-ratio 构造普通 CrossCoder 的伪分区，用来区分 DFC 的结构约束是否真的改变了特征几何。","experiments":"实验结果的核心是“DFC 能定位强 RL 特征，但不是完整的能力容器”。在 48 个变体的扫参中，把 RL 模型激活经过 crosscoder encode-decode 后，tool correctness 平均提升 +31.1±9.7 个百分点；把 base 模型激活经过 jointly-trained crosscoder，也能在没有微调的情况下被动转移 +6.8 个百分点工具调用正确率。这说明字典和解码路径中包含了可迁移的工具调用结构。DFC 的 A-exclusive 分区确实聚集了强模型特异性特征；但当对 exclusive 分区加惩罚时，RL 模型重构保真度下降，说明这些特征更像 filter，而不是完全吸收能力差异的 sink。最显著的行为控制结果是：只 steering 一个 A-exclusive 特征，就能带来 +65.0 个百分点的 tool-correctness 变化，并且在多数被探测层上泛化。几何分析显示，在 DFC 中 A-exclusive 特征形成可分离的 “Tool Interaction” 区域，区别于 shared 和 B-exclusive 特征；而同等规模的普通 CrossCoder 伪分区没有同样清晰的结构。评测使用 ToolRL held-out prompts：每个 sweep variant 100 条，每个 steering cell 40 条，greedy decoding，max_new_tokens=200，max_length=2048。指标包括 format_accuracy、tool_correctness 和取值为 {-1,0,+1,+2} 的 overall_score。","reading_guide":"建议精读，预计 60 到 90 分钟。先看 reconstruction、spillover 和 exclusive penalty 消融，这是判断 DFC 是 filter 还是 sink 的核心证据。最后看 Sec. 6 的单特征 steering，关注实验是否只改变工具调用格式，还是也改善了工具名选择。读时要特别留意三个限制：评测 prompt 数量较小；工具调用任务较窄；crosscoder 本身可能引入能力转移路径，因此“单特征定位”不等于该能力在原模型中只由一个神经元实现。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies how RL fine-tuning writes tool-use behavior into a language model’s internal representations. More precisely, it asks whether the capability difference between a base model and an RL-tuned tool-use model can be isolated into a small set of crosscoder features, whether those features fully carry the capability or only filter it, and whether they can be used for behavioral control without retraining.","importance":"This is a strong mechanistic study for the pretrain-to-posttrain interface. It moves the analysis of RL-induced agentic behavior from surface metrics to residual-stream features. The main implication is that post-training can create model-specific directions that are localizable and steerable. This matters for continual training, capability preservation, model diffing, model merging, safety switches, and runtime control. The result should be read with scope in mind: it is demonstrated on Qwen2.5-3B, a ToolRL-style tool-calling task, limited held-out evaluation, and a specific crosscoder setup. Then read the feature-space analysis, especially the mass-ratio control against a standard CrossCoder. The reconstruction, spillover, and exclusive-penalty ablations in Section 5 are the main evidence for the filter-versus-sink interpretation. The single-feature steering section is the most interesting behavior-control result; check whether the intervention improves valid tool selection or only pushes the model toward tool-call formatting. An important property of knowledge bases is that different queries for the same fact return consistent results, drawing on a single source of truth. We investigate whether LMs satisfy this property through behavioral and mechanistic analyses. Our results suggest that they encode knowledge in a task-specific manner. Behaviorally, facts ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-25T16:22:43Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2606_27237.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文要回答的问题很直接：语言模型参数里的“知识”是否像知识库那样具有 task-invariant 的单一事实表示。也就是，同一个事实被不同任务形式查询时，模型是否调用同一份内部存储。作者用行为分析和机制分析给出的答案是否定的：同一事实往往以任务相关的方式被编码，知识与任务格式在参数空间里纠缠，而不是干净地共享一个 source of truth。","importance":"它对预训练研究的启发不在于提出新训练方法，而在于修正一个常被默认接受的认知：把 LM 参数直接类比成统一知识库。这个结论会影响几个方向。第一，知识编辑、遗忘、数据删除这类 post-train 干预，可能只改掉某一种任务形式下的表现，别的格式仍然保留。第二，单任务 factual eval 可能严重低估或误判模型实际掌握的知识。第三，CoT 为什么有时能“找回”直接问答拿不到的知识，可能不是因为纯推理增强，而是激活了别的任务路径上的参数编码。对预训练侧来说，这提示训练目标和任务格式会塑造知识的内部组织方式，而不是只影响输出接口。","technical_approach":"方法分两部分。第一部分是行为层面的 co-emergence 分析。作者在 OLMo-3-7B IT 的训练 checkpoints 上追踪单个事实在不同任务上的“出现步数” E(f,t)。如果知识是任务无关共享存储，那么当模型已经具备相关任务能力后，一个事实一旦在某个任务上学会，应该会在其他任务上近乎同步出现。作者用 5 类关系数据、共 230 个事实，每个事实用 6 种任务形式 probing：Completion、FiTB、OpenQA、MCQA、Neg-MCQA、Verification，并为每个 dataset-task 设计 10 个 prompt paraphrase。判别类任务还做了选项轮换或真假配对，尽量减少模板偶然性。结果显示事实在不同任务上的 emergence 并不同步，且差异具有系统性。\n\n第二部分是机制层面的参数定位。作者基于 Bayazit 等人的 localization 框架，为每个 (fact, task) 对寻找一小组稀疏参数：移除这些参数后，该事实在该任务上的表现明显下降，但对同任务其他事实、或同事实其他任务影响较小。如果知识是 task-invariant 的，就不应能稳定找到这种 task-specific 的参数子集。论文在三个模型、五个关系数据集上都找到了这类局部子网络，说明同一事实的不同任务调用路径在参数上可以部分分离。\n\n在此基础上，作者又定义了 separability 指标来量化不同 (fact, task) 编码之间的重叠程度，并比较不同任务类型。一个重要发现是判别任务，尤其 MCQA 一类，更容易与其他编码纠缠；生成任务如 FiTB、Completion 的编码更可分离。另一个发现是通过生成任务学到的事实更容易在其他任务上共同出现，反过来则不成立。最后，作者把这个分析延伸到 CoT：当移除某任务自身的局部编码后，CoT 能恢复相当一部分性能；但当移除其他任务的编码时，CoT 比 direct answering 掉得更多。这说明 CoT 部分依赖跨任务的替代性知识路径。","experiments":"实验设置相当扎实。行为分析基于 OLMo-3-7B IT 的训练过程，覆盖 230 个事实、5 种关系、6 类任务、每类 10 个 paraphrase。作者不是只看最终准确率，而是跟踪每个事实在不同任务上的 emergence step，这比静态评测更能说明知识形成过程。主要结果是：事实跨任务共同出现的比例有限，很多事实先在某一种任务格式上出现，之后很久才在别的格式上稳定可用，甚至始终不转移。\n\n机制分析覆盖三个模型和五个关系数据集。作者能为大量 (fact, task) 对定位到稀疏参数子集，并验证这些子集具有选择性：删掉后主要伤害目标 pair，而不是普遍伤害整个 relation 或整个 task。论文还报告了 separability 的任务差异：MCQA、Verification 这类判别任务更 entangled，FiTB、Completion 这类生成任务更 separable。关于 CoT，关键结果是它对“其他任务编码”的依赖更强：移除非当前任务的局部编码时，CoT 的性能下降幅度超过 direct answer，支持了“CoT 会调动额外参数路径”的解释。\n\n当前给出的全文片段没有包含完整数值表，因此不适合编造具体百分比或 drop 数字。精读时建议重点核对：1）co-emergence 的定义阈值和稳健性；2）localization 的稀疏度、优化目标与对照实验；3）不同模型间结论是否一致；4）prompt paraphrase 与选项轮换是否足以排除模板偏置；5）CoT 恢复效应在不同关系和任务上是否稳定。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读但要抓住方法细节。先看 Introduction，明确作者挑战的是“LM 参数像统一知识库”这个类比。然后直接看行为实验部分，重点理解 emergence step、co-emergence 假设和 6 类任务设计。接着看 mechanistic localization 的定义与验证方式，这是全文最有信息量的部分。最后看 CoT 分析，因为它把 interpretability 结果和推理现象联系了起来。\n\n如果时间有限，优先看四处：任务与数据构造图、co-emergence 主图、localization 结果图、CoT 消融图。预计 30 分钟能完成一次有效泛读。值得深挖的点有两个：一是 task-specific encoding 到底反映预训练目标差异，还是 instruction tuning / evaluation format 放大的结果；二是这种碎片化知识表示对知识编辑、unlearning、continual pretrain 会带来什么约束。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether factual knowledge in language models is stored in a task-invariant way, as the common “LM-as-knowledge-base” analogy would suggest. If a fact is truly represented as a single internal source of truth, then different query formats should retrieve the same underlying representation. The paper argues this is not how current LMs work. Instead, the same fact is often encoded in a task-specific manner, with knowledge and task format intertwined in parameter space.","importance":"The contribution is conceptual but practically relevant. It challenges a simplifying assumption that often sits behind factual evaluation, model editing, and unlearning work: that a fact lives in one place and different prompts merely access it differently. If knowledge is task-specific, then editing a fact in one format may leave other formats intact, single-task factual benchmarks may give a partial picture, and some of the apparent benefit of chain-of-thought may come from activating alternative task-linked encodings rather than from pure reasoning. For pretraining research, the implication is that training objectives and task formats shape how knowledge is internally organized, not just how it is expressed at inference time.","technical_approach":"$3a","experiments":"$3b","reading_guide":"This is a good skim paper, but the method details matter. After that, go to the localization section; this is the most informative part of the paper because it turns a behavioral observation into a mechanistic claim. This surface similarity hides an opposite pattern within items. When an LRM gets a problem wrong, it spends more tokens than when it gets the same problem right; humans do the reverse, spending less time on the trials they get wrong. We separate two levels of deliberation: how response time tracks difficulty across items (registration), and, with item identity held fixed, whether an agent spends more on its own fail","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-25T01:21:53Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2606_26502.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作要区分两件常被混在一起的事：模型是否能识别哪些题更难，以及模型在自己即将答对或答错时如何分配推理预算。已有结果显示，LRM 的 reasoning token 长度和人类反应时在题目之间相关，但这只能说明难度登记相似，不能说明停止思考和继续思考的策略相似。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接贡献有限，但对 reasoning model 的训练和评测有启发。它提醒我们，长 CoT 或长 reasoning trace 不一定表示有效计算，可能只是模型在错误路径上持续展开。这个区分会影响 test-time scaling、推理长度奖励、RL 训练中的 token budget 设计，以及 verifier / early stopping 策略。若后训练阶段简单奖励“多想一点”，可能会强化一种与人类元认知相反的失败分配模式。","technical_approach":"方法核心是把 deliberation 拆成两个层次。第一层是 cross-item difficulty registration：不同题目之间，越难的题是否引发更长思考。第二层是 within-item deliberation allocation：固定题目身份后，agent 在自己答对和答错的试次上分别花多少思考资源。作者使用公开的人类与 LRM 匹配语料，将人类反应时和模型 reasoning token 长度分别在各自尺度内标准化，避免直接比较秒数和 token 数。然后引入 item fixed effects，吸收题目难度差异，再看 correctness 对 deliberation 的条件效应，以及人类和模型之间的交互。H-ARC 是主分析任务，INTUIT 用作结构不同的复现任务，Cortes 作为边界案例。模型侧包含多个开源 thinking LRM，并以 DeepSeek-V3 作为非 thinking baseline。一个重要限制是公开数据中每个 item × model 只有一个观测，因此 LRM 的 item-controlled slope 更多是跨模型识别，而不是同一模型多次采样下的稳定估计。","experiments":"关键结果是方向相反。跨题目看，人类和 LRM 都能复现已有的难度登记现象：难题对应更长反应时或更长 reasoning trace。固定题目后，人类在答错时往往更早 disengage，答对时花更长时间；LRM 则相反，答错时生成更多 reasoning token。H-ARC 上所有 thinking LRM 都表现出较大的 wrong-vs-right token effect，Cohen’s d 约为 1.47 到 3.13。INTUIT 上观察到类似分离，Cortes 则显示这种现象有任务边界。结果说明，LRM registers which items are hard, and how it allocates deliberation conditional on succeeding or failing on the same item. Prior human–LRM comparisons showed that reasoning-token length correlates with human reaction time across items. This study asks whether that cross-item alignment survives a within-item, outcome-conditioned test.","importance":"The result is not a pretraining recipe, but it is relevant for reasoning-model training and evaluation. It shows that longer reasoning traces can be concentrated on failures rather than successes. That matters for test-time compute scaling, reward design for chain-of-thought length, RL policies that trade accuracy against token budget, and early-stopping mechanisms. A training signal that rewards more visible reasoning may reinforce persistence on unproductive trajectories unless it is paired with confidence, verification, or stopping signals.","technical_approach":"$3c","experiments":"The main empirical pattern is a sign reversal. On H-ARC, all thinking LRMs show a large wrong-versus-right effect, with Cohen’s d roughly between 1.47 and 3.13. INTUIT gives a similar replication, while Cortes marks a task-dependent boundary. s","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-25T08:33:34Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2606_26749.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的是一个训练动力学层面的基础问题：语言模型在几乎 one-hot 的 next-token supervision 下，为什么还能学到有语义结构的表示。按 Neural Collapse（NC）理论，在平衡的一对一标签监督下，最终表示应收敛到由标签决定的 simplex ETF 几何，类别之间等距，输入语义相似性会被抹平。但真实语言模型显然会形成语义、句法、潜在类别等结构。论文要回答的不是“LM 会不会学到结构”，而是更细的两个问题：这种结构在严格 one-hot 监督下是如何被梯度下降找到的；以及它为什么能出现，却又不违背 NC 对终态的预测。","importance":"对预训练研究的价值在于，它把一个常被经验性接受的事实——next-token 训练会形成语义几何——拆成了“早期训练动力学”和“终态几何”两个阶段。这个区分很重要。它提示我们，很多对表征有用的结构可能不是优化终点的性质，而是训练过程中的暂态产物。若这个结论在更真实的语料和更大模型上继续成立，那么它会直接影响几个方向：一是如何理解 cooldown、early stopping、mid-training recipe 对表示质量的影响；二是为什么某些后期继续训练会让表示更任务化但未必更通用；三是如何设计损失、数据混合或正则，让模型保留早期形成的语义几何，而不是完全滑向标签主导的 collapse。贡献类型偏机制分析和训练理论接口，不是直接给 recipe，但问题意识很扎实。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者没有直接在真实大语料上做不可控分析，而是构造了三类 synthetic toy languages，让“潜在语义结构存在，但监督严格 one-hot”这件事可被精确追踪。每个样本只做一次 next-word prediction，而不是对整段序列所有位置做 loss，这样可以明确保证 context-label 分布是一对一的 one-hot 监督。三套 toy language 从简单到更接近语言，核心共同点是：不同 context 共享某些潜在语义因子，但训练标签仍是唯一 token。\n\n为了测量模型到底学到了什么几何，作者引入了 Representational Similarity Analysis（RSA）。他们先根据 toy language 的潜在语义类别构造一个“语义相似性目标 Gram matrix”，再把模型上下文表示的相似性矩阵与之比较；同时也与 NC 预测的 ETF 几何进行对照。这样就能沿训练过程跟踪：表示是更接近潜在语义结构，还是更接近标签驱动的等距几何。\n\n实验上训练的是一组标准 decoder-only Transformer，并系统扫宽度、深度等容量因素，观察训练轨迹而不是只看最终点。论文的关键发现是：即便监督严格 one-hot，梯度下降在训练早期仍会先恢复由共享潜在因子组织起来的语义几何；但当模型容量足够、训练时间足够长时，这种结构会逐步让位于 ETF 式的 terminal geometry。也就是说，语义结构不是 NC 的反例，而是 NC 终态之前的 transient phase。\n\n作者还给了一个简化数学模型，用来复现实验中观察到的“先语义对齐、后 collapse”的轨迹。这个部分的价值不在于完全刻画真实 LM，而在于提供一个可分析的最小机制：梯度下降会先利用样本间共享因子带来的方向性信号，随后在长期优化中被 one-hot 标签几何牵引到更对称的终态。\n\n和已有工作相比，这篇论文的区别不在于再证明一次 NC，也不在于讨论 soft-label/label co-occurrence 如何产生语义，而是专门研究“没有显式 label bridge 时，语义几何为何仍会短暂出现”。这个切口比较新，也更贴近长上下文 LM 的现实，因为长 context 下重复上下文几乎消失，经典的共享标签桥接解释不够用。","experiments":"从全文片段能确认的实验结论有四点。\n\n第一，三种 synthetic language 上都观察到相同现象：表示在训练早期与潜在语义类别的 RSA 对齐度上升，随后在继续训练中下降，而与 ETF 几何的一致性上升。这说明“语义几何先出现、后坍缩”不是单一 toy setup 的偶然结果。\n\n第二，作者扫了不同深度和 embedding 维度，结论是模型容量和训练时长决定是否会明显走到 terminal ETF。容量更大、训练更久时，NC 终态更容易显现；容量有限或训练不足时，模型可能停留在带语义结构的中间阶段。这一点对实际大模型训练很关键，因为工业训练往往并不真的到达某种理论终态。\n\n第三，论文强调所有实验都采用严格 one-hot 的单位置预测设置，而不是 full next-token loss。这使得结果更干净：语义结构的出现不能被“同一 context 对应多个可能标签”的软监督解释掉。\n\n第四，作者给出的简化数学模型在经验和理论上都能复现 transient semantic alignment 的轨迹，说明这个现象不是纯经验观察，而是有可分析支撑。\n\n当前提供的全文片段没有具体表格、模型层数、维度、训练步数和 RSA 数值曲线，因此不能安全填写更细的数字。但如果精读正文，最值得抄下来的应是：各 toy language 的定义、RSA 指标构造方式、不同容量下语义对齐峰值出现的训练阶段、以及 ETF 对齐何时反超语义对齐。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合做机制分析导向的泛读到精读之间。建议这样看：\n1. 先读 Introduction，抓住论文真正要解决的矛盾：one-hot supervision 按 NC 理论应抹平语义，但 LM 训练中语义结构又确实存在。\n2. 接着看 synthetic paper studies a basic optimization question: how do language models acquire semantically structured representations under effectively one-hot next-token supervision? Neural Collapse (NC) theory says that under balanced one-hot supervision, representations should converge to a simplex ETF geometry determined only by labels, with no trace of input semantics. Yet language models clearly learn semantic, syntactic, and latent-category structure. The paper is not asking whether LMs learn structure in practice. It asks a sharper pair of questions: how gradient descent can recover semantic geometry when supervision is strictly one-hot, and why this does not necessarily contradict NC if NC describes the terminal state rather than the full training trajectory.","importance":"This matters for pretraining because it reframes representation quality as a question of training dynamics, not just asymptotic optimization. The main message is that useful semantic geometry may be a transient phase rather than a property of the final optimum. If that picture extends beyond toy settings, it has implications for cooldown schedules, early stopping, continual pretraining, and the common observation that longer optimization can make representations more task-shaped but not always more generally useful. It also suggests a concrete research direction: losses, curricula, or regularizers that preserve early semantic organization instead of letting optimization drift all the way toward label-dominated collapse. The paper frames a deployment failure mode as compositional behavioral leakage and connects it to the lack of architectural isolation in standard Transformer attention. Markdown headings, delimiters, and templates are conventions, not scope boundaries. This is relevant to research on long-context behavior, instruction hierarchy, prompt modularity, and whether pretraining or alignment can teach models more reliable notions of scope.","technical_approach":"The main contribution is an operational definition and a reusable perturbation protocol. Given a module set M, a focal module mi, and an edit e applied to a non-focal module mj, CBL occurs when the edit is not intended to affect the focal behavior but induces a detectable paired shift in the focal score si(x; M). The protocol perturbs non-focal modules along three channels: volume, content, and form. The paper also separates CBL from prompt injection, compositional privacy leakage, and longitudinal degradation: the issue here is accidental interference at initialization time inside a shared attention context.","experiments":"The empirical probe is run on a deployed job-evaluation agent using Claude Sonnet 4.6, with 12 job descriptions and 144 trials. The volume and form channels do not show a similarly clear paired effect. The authors call this within-item discrepancy the Inattentional Gap.","importance":"This is not a direct pretraining contribution, but it is relevant to how foundation models are evaluated and deployed. Many benchmarks only ask whether the specified task is solved, not whether task conditioning suppresses other critical information that the model could have surfaced. For base-model researchers, the paper is a reminder that instruction following, output scoping, and broad situational reporting can conflict, and that this tension may need explicit treatment in post-training or evaluation design.","technical_approach":"The paper operationalizes a machine analogue of human inattentional blindness. The key setup is a paired comparison on the same input and the same model under two prompt conditions: an open condition that allows unconstrained reporting, and a task-conditioned prompt that narrows the reporting frame. Crucially, the gap is estimated only on items where the model does report the critical signal in the open condition. That filters out pure capability failures and isolates task-induced omission. The study spans both language and vision settings, uses safety-relevant scenarios, and analyzes how the gap varies with task load, signal salience, model family, scale, and output-scope constraints.","experiments":"The main findings are that the Inattentional Gap appears in both language and vision models, is not removed simply by scaling up models or adding reasoning capability, differs across modalities and model families, and is strongly linked to output-scope narrowing. The important methodological point is that this is not framed as a generic failure-to-detect result, because the analysis conditions on open-condition reportability. The provided excerpt does not include the full result tables or exact numbers, but the introduction makes clear that the study is systematic across modalities, domains, and model scales.","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper for a pretraining-focused reader. 不可识别性（Theorem 3.2）：构造存在两任务在 probe set 上激活表示完全相同（Zi=Zj），但输出头梯度/更新方向正交，从而 head Fisher alignment 为 0。直观原因是 head 更新不仅取决于激活 a，还取决于 logit-space 的误差/残差 e（与标签/目标 token 强相关）；只看 a 会丢掉“误差几何”。论文给了两类实例：\n- joint (a,e) 特征分布的 kernel mean embedding 之间的余弦相似度。这个“lift”把 Fisher 矩阵比较变成了分布嵌入的余弦，并且显式分解为 activation 几何、error 几何及二者耦合项。\n3) FisherSketch：为避免显式构造 K×K 的误差二阶矩（Γe=E[ee^T]）和每样本 O(mK) 的投影开销，提出一遍流式（one-pass activation steering vectors derived from binary contrastive sample pairs suffer from three key limitations: they cannot produce decomposable, precise quantification of behavioral intensity, they are semantically opaque and offer no insight into constituent sub-components of a behavior, and they require manipulation of the full residual stream, which often entangles with unrelated capabilities and degrades general model performance. Existing sparse autoencoder (SAE) based feature approaches mostly treat behavioral features as binary triggers, and do not support continuous behavioral measurement or stable, low-side-effect intervention. For alignment pathologies such as sycophancy, there remains a lack of interpretable, controllable intervention methods.","importance":"The cascading feature selection framework introduced in this work provides a more stable alternative to standard activation addition for post-training intervention and mechanistic interpretability. The core idea of selecting causal features based on monotonic activation scaling across behavioral intensity generalizes to other alignment-induced behaviors including hallucination and over-refusal. It also adds empirical evidence for how behavioral traits introduced during SFT and RLHF are distributed in activation space, enabling low-damage behavior modification at inference time without retraining.","technical_approach":"$41","experiments":"Validation is conducted on sycophancy detection and control tasks using Llama 3.1 8B, with comparisons to standard dense steering baselines including Activation Addition and Contrastive Activation Addition. Identified sycophancy features form a linearly separable subspace in activation space, supporting robust detection and intervention across diverse contexts. 的排序相关性。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The work asks whether standard uncertainty-evaluation metrics actually support downstream decisions, rather than merely optimizing generic surrogates such as negative log-likelihood or expected calibration error.","importance":"The connection to foundation-model research is indirect but useful. Modern LLM pipelines rely on reward models, verifiers, judges, selective prediction, abstention policies, and risk-aware routing. All of these depend on uncertainty estimates. The main lesson is that calibration or likelihood metrics can rank models differently from the utility that matters in deployment. If a metric encodes an unsuitable implicit prior over downstream decisions, it can push method development in the wrong direction.","technical_approach":"The central notion is decision-alignment. For a family of downstream utilities U_theta, a metric is decision-aligned if there exists a prior over theta and a strictly increasing transformation, for each fixed label set, such that the metric ranks predictions exactly as expected downstream utility under that prior. This turns metric analysis into an analysis of implicit decision beliefs. The work proves an equivalence: decision-alignment is the same as strict order preservation and tie preservation with respect to the corresponding utility family. The framework is then used to analyze common uncertainty metrics, showing that some either fail to correspond to any coherent decision belief or encode pathological priors. The proposed alternative is prior-weighted utility metrics, a class of proper scoring rules that evaluates probabilistic predictions through an explicit prior over downstream utilities.","experiments":"The empirical section covers classification and regression benchmarks plus real-world case studies. The reported pattern is that prior-weighted utility metrics align more consistently with actual downstream utilities, while common metrics such as NLL and ECE can produce model rankings that do not match decision value. The provided content does not include full benchmark names, tables, or numerical effect sizes, so the reliable takeaway is the evaluation criterion and the failure modes of standard metrics rather than a specific numeric improvement.","reading_guide":"A 30–40 minute skim is appropriate. The section on prior-weighted utility metrics is the most transferable part for LLM work, especially reward-model calibration, verifier confidence, and judge reliability. We show that this can miss vulnerabilities introduced by fine-tuning itself: models can learn token-level indicator semantics that preserve canonical accuracy while failing under behavior-preserving transformations such as PowerShell alias substitution, command reconstruction, string construction, execution indirection, and case mutation. We study Foundation-S","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-25T14:29:03Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2606_27091.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CR","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是一个很具体、但对安全场景很麻烦的问题：同一个基座模型在安全分类任务上经过微调后，表面上的标准测试集准确率会提升，但模型可能同时学到一套对“规范写法”过度依赖的判别语义，导致它在语义不变、行为不变、只是写法被改写的脚本上出现系统性漏检。作者关心的不是一般意义上的分布外鲁棒性，而是微调本身是否把原模型内部已有的判别回路重新加权，变成一种对指示 token 敏感、对变形脆弱的分类器。更具体地说，在 PowerShell 恶意脚本检测里，像 Invoke-WebRequest、Invoke-Expression 这类命令家族既是语义线索，也是攻击者最容易做别名替换、字符串重构、大小写扰动的部位。论文要解决的是：能不能在部署前、不先枚举大量逃逸样本的情况下，仅凭规范输入上的内部表征变化，提前发现哪些命令家族已经成为高风险逃逸面。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接启发不在“安全分类”这个应用，而在“微调如何改写基座模型已有电路”这件事。文章给出的结论很清楚：Foundation-Sec 这类安全微调模型并没有重新长出一套全新的 PowerShell 检测器，而是继承了 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 里已有的分类路径，并在此基础上做了集中化和语义专门化。这样做能提高规范分布上的分类表现，但也可能把原本较稳健的判别信号压缩成更窄的 token 级语义依赖。这个观察对更广义的 post-train / continual pretrain 都有参考意义：当下游目标很强、数据分布又有明显表面相关性时，训练往往不是“增加能力”，而是“重配已有能力的使用方式”，而这种重配可能牺牲变形鲁棒性。论文的贡献量级不在提出通用训练新范式，而在给出一个可操作的、机制层面的检测框架：用基座-微调模型对照、token 消融符号反转、线性 probe 排名，去定位微调引入的家族级脆弱点。这对研究 SFT/RL 是否扭曲预训练表征、以及如何做部署前红队优先级排序，都有现实价值。","technical_approach":"方法上有三层。第一层是机制分析。作者把 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 作为基座对照，把 Foundation-Sec-8B-Instruct 作为安全微调后的模型，比较两者在 PowerShell 分类上的内部表示与因果路径。核心判断是：微调后的模型继承了基座中已有的中层分类回路，尤其是文中强调的 Layer 12 附近的判别路径，而不是新建一条完全不同的检测路线。作者用因果干预和表示分析去验证这一点，重点不是简单看 attention map，而是看替换/消融某些内部激活后输出置信度如何变化。\n\n第二层是预部署检测信号。作者提出 indicator-token sign test：对一组规范恶意脚本，找到命令家族中的指示 token，例如 Invoke-WebRequest；然后在输入中把这些 token 做消融，比较消融前后恶意分类置信度的变化方向。若在基座模型中，去掉该 token 会降低恶意置信度，说明它仍是“driver”；但在微调模型中，去掉它反而提高恶意置信度，说明它已变成“suppressor”。这种符号反转意味着模型对该家族的判别主要转移到了周围 payload 上，而显式命令词本身在规范样本里反而起到抑制作用。作者把这解释为一种危险信号：一旦攻击者把显式命令词换成别名、格式串重构或大小写扰动，模型可能失去稳定锚点。\n\n第三层是家族级排序与验证。作者没有试图做逐脚本的逃逸预测，而是用线性 probe 在分类边界附近测量表征漂移，再结合 sign test，把命令家族按风险排序，输出给红队做定向测试。随后他们构建三层逃逸 benchmark：直接改写、命令重构、大小写变异，并要求种子脚本与变体脚本在语法和行为上保持一致。这个设计的好处是把“模型怕不怕 obfuscation”拆成可归因的变换类型，而不是混成一个模糊的鲁棒性分数。\n\n和常见安全评测不同，这篇文章的关键不是再造一个更大的攻击集，而是提出一种从内部表征出发的前置监测方法。和一般 OOD 检测也不同，它利用的是基座-微调配对以及 token 因果角色变化，而不是只看置信度或 embedding 距离。","experiments":"实验围绕一个 293 对的匹配脚本 cohort 和三层逃逸基准展开，比较对象是 Foundation-Sec-8B-Instruct 与其同源基座 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct。文章最关键的结果有三点。\n\n第一，indicator-token sign test 在规范输入上就能提前指出高风险家族。作者发现 Invoke-WebRequest 家族的符号反转最明显，随后在真实逃逸测试中，这一家族确实在 iwr 别名替换上出现稳定漏检。也就是说，风险排序不是事后解释，而是对后续红队结果有预测力。\n\n第二，微调模型的失败是“变换敏感”的，而不是普遍分类能力差。Foundation-Sec 在规范 PowerShell 分类上优于基座，文中也引用其先前工作在 threat-hunting workflow 中相对 baseline 有约 +16% precision 提升；但在逃逸基准上，它会在多类行为保持不变的改写中漏掉恶意脚本，包括 Invoke-Expression 的 format-string reconstruction、alternating-case Invoke-Expression，以及 alternating-case IEX alias 等。相对地，Llama 在同一批评测变体上是 zero misses。这个对照很重要，因为它说明问题不是“基座模型本来就不会”，而是“微调后丢掉了原有的某种鲁棒性”。\n\n第三，作者还观察到 prompt-level remediation 很不稳定：针对某一命令家族加提示或补丁，可能修复一个家族，却打开或放大另一个家族的漏检。这说明问题不只是 prompt 层面的表面偏置，而是内部判别语义已经被重排。虽然全文片段里没有给出所有消融表格的具体数值，但从叙述看，作者做了至少三类验证：家族级 sign test 排名、三层变换基准、以及内部因果干预。若精读正文，应重点找 Layer 12 路径替换实验、线性 probe 的 family ranking 结果，以及不同变换类型下的 miss breakdown。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合按“问题定义—机制证据—检测方法—基准验证”四步读。\n\n先看 Introduction，抓住文章真正的 claim：不是在说所有安全微调都会降低鲁棒性，而是在说标准 held-out evaluation 会漏掉一种由微调诱发的 transformation-sensitive evasion。这里要特别记住 driver/suppressor 这组概念，因为后面的方法都围绕它展开。\n\n然后看机制分析部分，重点找作者如何证明 Foundation-Sec 继承的是 Llama 的已有分类回路，而不是新学出一条检测器。凡是涉及 activation patching、causal intervention、layer-wise effect 的图表都值得细看，尤其是 Layer 12 附近的证据。如果这里只是相关性而非因果性，结论会弱很多；作者显然在努力把它做成因果论证。\n\n接着看 sign test 和线性 probe 的方法定义。这里要关注两个问题：一是 indicator token 如何选；二是“符号反转”为什么足以作为家族级风险信号，而不是偶然的 token artifact。若正文里有 family-level ranking 表或 ROC/precision 类指标，建议仔细看，因为这决定了方法是否真能用于部署前筛查。\n\n最后看 benchmark 构造与失败案例。建议重点看 Invoke-WebRequest / iwr、Invoke-Expression 格式串重构、大小写扰动这几组。它们代表了三类不同难度的变换：直接别名、结构性重写、表面形态扰动。若时间有限，读 Introduction、方法里 sign test 定义、以及实验中 family-level case study，大约 25–35 分钟可以抓住主线；若要深入理解微调如何重排内部语义，最好把机制分析章节和附录中的干预细节一起看。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a specific failure mode of fine-tuned security classifiers: a model can improve on standard held-out evaluation while becoming easier to evade under behavior-preserving transformations. The setting is PowerShell malware classification. Commands such as Invoke-WebRequest or Invoke-Expression are strong indicators in canonical scripts, but they are also easy for attackers to rewrite through aliases, string reconstruction, indirection, or case mutation. The central question is not generic OOD robustness. It is whether fine-tuning itself reshapes inherited classifier circuitry so that the model relies on narrow token-level semantics that look fine on canonical data but break under cheap rewrites. The practical goal is pre-deployment detection: can one identify vulnerable command families from canonical inputs alone, before generating a large evasion set?","importance":"Its value for pretraining researchers comes from the mechanism, not from the security application alone. The paper argues that the fine-tuned model does not build a new PowerShell detector from scratch. Instead, it reuses an inherited classification route from the base model and semantically specializes it. That specialization improves canonical task performance but can reduce transformation robustness. This is a useful lens for post-training and continual pretraining more broadly: downstream adaptation often reallocates existing internal circuits rather than adding new capability, and that reallocation can create brittle dependencies on surface-form cues. The contribution is not a new training recipe. It is a concrete diagnostic framework based on base-vs-finetuned comparison, token-ablation sign reversal, and family-level ranking. That makes it relevant to anyone studying how SFT or domain adaptation distorts pretrained representations and how to prioritize red-teaming before deployment.","technical_approach":"$44","experiments":"$45","reading_guide":"$46","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看§4因果干预实验，微调复用预训练电路而非新建的结论，为后训练安全与电路复用假设提供干净证据","en":"Read only §4 causal intervention experiments; the conclusion that fine-tuning reuses rather than builds new pretraining circuits provides clean evidence for post-training safety and circuit reuse hypotheses"},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Ryan Fetterman"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["interpretability","fine-tuning","circuit analysis","llm security","causal intervention"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27091","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.27091","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.27321","title":"Beyond the Hard Budget: Sparsity Regularizers for More Interpretable Top-k Sparse Autoencoders","abstract":"Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become a leading tool for interpreting the representations of vision foundation models, decomposing their polysemantic activations into a larger set of sparse, more monosemantic features. If the full transition graph were known, task solving would reduce to graph search; in the agent setting, only successful traces are observed. Skill-DisCo approximates this missing graph structure through a Parameterized Finite-State Machine view. It lifts concrete states and actions into parameterized states and operators, searches for compact high-coverage control-flow subgraphs across traces, and then compiles these discoveries into callable, executable, and verifiable procedural skills. base-model pretraining, but it is relevant to post-training loops, agentic RL, synthetic data generation, and learned evaluation. The central issue is also present in reward models, AI judges, verifier training, and reasoning-data pipelines: a fixed evaluator can be overfit, while a freely changing evaluator makes optimization unstable.","technical_approach":"RQGM divides search into evaluator epochs. Within each epoch, the evaluator is frozen, so the task-agent search still optimizes a stationary utility. In parallel, challenger evaluators are evolved and tested on held-out ground-truth data. A challenger replaces the incumbent only if it statistically improves an ε-best-belief score. After replacement, the system applies selective erasure: it removes only archived utility records that depended on the displaced evaluator. The main design choice is to permit non-stationary utilities only at controlled epoch boundaries, rather than letting the reward signal drift continuously.","experiments":"The empirical study covers coding, paper writing/reviewing, and proof writing/grading.
